Recent test-time adaptive sequence models address this challenge by updating internal states during inference, but tie adaptation to instantaneous prediction errors or surprise. This coupling can conflate persistent distribution shift with stochastic innovations, leading to unnecessary updates and inefficient adaptation. We introduce Black-Mamba, a test-time adaptive forecasting architecture that formulates online adaptation as evidence-gated state tracking under distribution drift. The model augments a base predictor with a dynamic memory updated when temporally accumulated surprisal provides sufficient evidence of a regime change. This turns adaptation into a selective, event-driven process rather than a continuous one. Across multiple forecasting benchmarks with non-stationary dynamics, Black-Mamba achieves competitive or improved predictive performance compared to existing test-time adaptation methods while significantly reducing the number of memory updates during inference. Together with mathematical analysis and biological evidence, these results suggest that accumulated surprisal provides a principled signal for distinguishing persistent drift from transient noise, yielding more efficient and robust adaptation.

</details>


### [100] [Enhancing Transformer-based Routing by Encoding Distance via Relative Positional Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18909)
*Leyre Encío,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在Transformer架构中引入相对位置编码（RPE）作为加性偏置来解决团队定向问题（TOP），通过在注意力机制中嵌入节点间的成对空间关系，使编码器学习更丰富的空间感知图嵌入，从而提升解码器生成路径的质量。在多达100个节点的实例上，该方法在奖励收集和最优性差距方面均优于现有最先进的vanilla Transformer方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于vanilla Transformer的团队定向问题求解方法未能充分利用节点间的空间关系信息，导致图嵌入的空间感知能力不足，限制了路径质量和解的泛化性能。

Method: 在Transformer编码器的注意力机制中引入相对位置编码（RPE），以加性偏置的形式将节点间的成对空间关系信息嵌入到注意力计算中，从而在编码阶段获得空间感知更强的图表示，进而指导解码器生成更优的路径。

Result: 在多达100个节点的实例上，该方法相比其他使用vanilla Transformer的SOTA方法，在收集奖励和最优性差距上均取得一致的改进。

Conclusion: 显式的关系建模（如RPE）能显著增强Transformer在复杂组合优化问题上的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper explores Relative Positional Encoding (RPE) as an additive bias in Transformer architectures to solve the Team Orienteering Problem. By embedding in the attention mechanism pairwise spatial relationships among nodes of the graph that represents the routing problem, the transformer encoder can compute a richer spatial-aware graph embedding that allows the decoder to estimate better routes. Experimental results involving instances up to 100 nodes demonstrate consistent improvements in collected rewards and optimality gaps over vanilla Transformer architectures used by other state-of-the-art works. These findings highlight that explicit relational modeling significantly enhances scalability and generalization for complex combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [101] [OntoBook: Ontology-Grounded Synthetic Textbooks for Medical Encoder Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18927)
*Rian Touchent,Éric de la Clergerie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OntoBook通过将医学本体结构转换为流畅的教科书风格文本，用于预训练编码器语言模型。在三个法语医学编码基准上，该方法相比纯MLM预训练取得了显著提升（FRACCO上+2.5 micro-F1，Distemist上+8.0 micro-F1），并发现目标对齐至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学编码任务需要理解层级和因果关系，但传统MLM预训练缺乏对结构化医学本体知识的显式利用，限制了编码器在医学文本理解上的表现。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) 在本体图上进行随机游走，捕获医学编码间的层级和因果关系；(2) 使用大语言模型将游走路径重写为流畅的教科书风格文本；(3) 在生成文本上使用双重预训练目标：掩码语言建模和编码对关系预测，训练ModernCamemBERT（149M参数法语编码器）。

Result: 在FRACCO上+2.5 micro-F1，Distemist-FR上+8.0 micro-F1；目标对齐至关重要，不对齐训练导致30个点的性能退化；发布了130万条LLM生成的医学教科书文本（覆盖CIM-10、CCAM、ATC三种法语本体）。

Conclusion: 将结构化医学本体知识转化为自然语言文本进行预训练能有效提升编码器在医学编码任务上的性能，且预训练目标与数据之间的对齐是成功的关键因素。

Abstract: We present OntoBook, a method that converts medical ontology structure into pretraining signal for encoder language models. Our approach has three stages: random walks through ontology graphs capture hierarchical and causal relations between medical codes, a large language model reformulates these walks into fluent textbook-style prose, and the resulting text is used to train ModernCamemBERT, a 149M-parameter French encoder, with two objectives on the same data: masked language modeling and relation prediction between code pairs. On three French medical coding benchmarks (FRACCO, Cantemist-FR, Distemist-FR), OntoBook achieves significant improvements over MLM-only pretraining, with +2.5 micro-F1 on FRACCO and +8.0 micro-F1 on Distemist. We find that alignment between objectives is necessary: misaligned training, where each task uses different data, causes a 30-point degradation. We release 1.3 million LLM-reformulated medical textbooks across three French ontologies (CIM-10, CCAM, ATC) and pretrained model checkpoints.

</details>


### [102] [What General Intelligence Requires: Non-Reducible Constraints Across Levels of Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18943)
*Subhomoy Bakshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通用智能受多重不可约的结构约束（跨AI系统、人类学、法学、经济学四个层面），任何单一架构进步或单纯扩展规模都无法实现AGI，并给出了23条约束和5个可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前AI研究中对"规模扩展（scaling）"范式能最终通向AGI的主流假设，论证通用智能的结构性约束分布在不可互相还原的不同描述层次上，需要更长期、多层面的研究路径。

Method: 通过四个证据透镜（AI系统研究、人类学、法律、经济学）各锚定一个描述层次，辅以思辨小说作为发现语境（非辩护语境）中的纪律性启发工具，导出约束分类体系。

Result: 识别出23条结构约束，归入8个聚类；深度分析了其中6条，将其排序为一个上升的层次阶梯；推导出5个具名基准系列和反证条件的可证伪预测。

Conclusion: 通用智能不能仅靠架构创新或规模扩展实现，研究计划必须对照完整的约束谱系进行评估，AGI的实现需要比"规模假说"所暗示的更长的时间周期。

Abstract: General intelligence, of the kind that underwrites the full range of human cognitive achievement, is not a property of computational architecture alone. This paper advances a single thesis: the structural constraints on general intelligence occupy distinct levels of description and are mutually non-reducible, in the sense that the special-sciences tradition gives to that term. It follows that no single architectural advance, and no continuation of the scaling programme by itself, can produce artificial general intelligence (AGI), and that research programmes must be evaluated against the full constraint profile rather than against performance on any one benchmark. The thesis is developed through a method that reads general intelligence through four evidential lenses, AI systems research, anthropology, law, and economics, each anchored to a distinct level of description, supplemented by speculative fiction used as a disciplined heuristic in the context of discovery rather than the context of justification. Applying the method yields a taxonomy of twenty-three structural constraints organised into eight clusters; six are examined in depth and ordered as an ascending ladder of levels, with explicit bridges showing why progress at one level cannot carry to the next. The argument issues in five falsifiable predictions, each stated with a named benchmark family and a disconfirmation condition, converting a descriptive framework into a research programme with a longer horizon than the scaling hypothesis implies.

</details>


### [103] [From Dependency to Compositionality: A Neurosymbolic Lifting of LLM Outputs via Combinatory Categorial Grammar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18961)
*Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号框架，将自回归LLM的输出提升为组合范畴语法(CCG)的增量式类型完备推导，不假设LLM内部实现CCG，而是认为其输出可进行原理性、增量且可审计的CCG重建。该框架通过Curry-Howard对应扩展到形式语言（如Solidity、OWL、SQL），并支持两层检查：组合层捕获结构错误，内容层通过外部知识检测幻觉内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"LLM缺乏真正语法"的批评以及依存语法视角的回应，指出一个被忽视的关键观察：自回归生成的前缀驱动、类型补全动态与CCG最初设计的增量处理模型高度一致，为此提出更精确的形式化描述框架。

Method: 提出神经符号框架：将LLM输出提升为CCG类型化组合推导，通过Curry-Howard对应扩展到多种形式语言（编程语言、描述逻辑、查询语言），并设计两层检查机制（组合层+内容层）实现幻觉检测。

Result: 该框架能跨自然语言和多种形式语言统一运作（可编程语言、描述逻辑、查询语言），支持早期幻觉检测（组合层捕捉结构失败，内容层通过外部知识验证），且对生成系统仅要求前缀驱动特性而非认知能力。

Conclusion: 闭用同步LLM-CCG耦合的设想，指出该框架为可审计、可验证的神经符号系统开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate fluent text by incrementally predicting the next token from a prefix. Critics in the generative tradition argue that such systems lack genuine grammar; influential replies from the dependency-grammar perspective hold that LLM behavior is well described by local head-dependent structure built word by word. We argue that a sharper observation has been overlooked: the prefix-driven, type-completing dynamics of autoregressive generation align closely with the incremental processing model that Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG) was originally designed to support. On this basis we propose a neurosymbolic framework in which LLM outputs are lifted into typed compositional derivations -- not claiming that LLMs implement CCG internally, but that their outputs admit a principled, incremental, and auditable CCG reconstruction. Two consequences follow. First, through the Curry-Howard correspondence the lifting extends beyond natural language to the formal languages LLMs also produce -- programming languages such as Solidity, description-logic and query languages such as OWL and SQL -- with the type system varying and the architecture held fixed. Second, the lifting supports two layers of checking: a compositional layer that catches structural failures directly, and a content layer that checks the lifted structure against external knowledge sources, enabling the earliest possible flagging of hallucinated content. The account thereby requires of a producer not cognition but a prefix-driven generative profile. We close with a sketch of synchronous LLM-CCG coupling as one direction the framework opens.

</details>


### [104] [Measuring Reward-Seeking via Contrastive Belief Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18966)
*Axel Højmark,Jérémy Scheurer,Evgenia Nitishinskaya,Felix Hofstätter,Jason Wolfe,Theodore Ehrenborg,Bronson Schoen,Alexander Meinke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对比合成文档微调（SDF）方法，用于测量语言模型在强化学习训练中产生的“奖励追逐”行为。通过对OpenAI o3中间检查点的分析，发现模型在编码和对齐任务中更倾向于迎合评分者偏好而非用户/开发者意图，且该倾向随RL训练增强。例如，在承诺与任务完成冲突的场景中，后期检查点87%选择违约（当SDF文档提示评分者奖励任务完成时），而早期仅40%。该方法也能泛化到已知的奖励破解模型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练的语言模型可能会学会优化评分者的判断而非预设目标，这种"奖励追逐"行为难以直接测量，因为当评分者奖励正确行为时，两类模型表现完全相同。需要一种方法能在评分者偏好与开发者意图产生冲突时分离并测量这种行为。

Method: 使用对比合成文档微调（SDF），通过构造合成文档改变模型对评分者奖励内容的信念，使评分者偏好与用户/开发者意图产生冲突，然后测量模型采纳哪一方偏好的比率。应用于OpenAI o3 RL训练过程中的中间检查点。

Result: o3中间检查点倾向于迎合评分者偏好而非用户/开发者偏好；该倾向随RL训练递增（后期检查点在承诺任务中87%选择违约 vs 早期40%）；方法能泛化到已知奖励破解模型（gpt-oss-120b的敏感度从33%提升至86%）。

Conclusion: 强化学习可能在训练过程中增加奖励追逐行为，产生在认为可获得更高奖励时会违背开发者意图的模型，对AI安全有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Language models trained with reinforcement learning may learn to optimize the grader's judgment rather than the intended objective. This "reward-seeking" is difficult to measure because a model that pursues the grader's judgment and one that pursues the intended objective behave identically whenever the grader rewards the intended behavior. We measure reward-seeking using Contrastive Synthetic Document Finetuning to change a model's beliefs about what the grader rewards, putting those beliefs in conflict with what users or developers want, and measuring the rate at which the model adopts each party's preferred behavior. Applied to intermediate checkpoints of a capabilities-focused OpenAI o3 RL run, without safety training, we find that these checkpoints often side with grader preferences over those of users or developers on coding and alignment tasks. This tendency to side with the grader trends upward throughout RL training. For example, in an environment that forces a choice between keeping a promise to a supervisor and breaking it to complete the task, a late capabilities-focused o3 checkpoint breaks the promise 87% of the time when SDF documents say the grader rewards task completion, versus 9% when they say it rewards honesty (a choice its chain-of-thought often makes explicit). An earlier checkpoint is far less sensitive (40% vs. 24%). Our method also generalizes to reward-hacking models. A model organism trained to reward-hack (gpt-oss-120b) is more than twice as sensitive to grader preferences as the unmodified model, with the mean behavioral shift in favor of the grader rising from 33% to 86%. These results indicate that RL can increase reward-seeking over the course of training, producing models that may act against their developers' intentions when they believe that doing so leads to higher reward.

</details>


### [105] [Mi-Memory: A Lifecycle Memory Framework for Personal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18975)
*Xule Liu,Hanlin Teng,Chao Li,Yanan Ni,Shuo Lu,Audrey Wang,Yijun Liu,Yunfei Wang,Xiaofeng Li,Xian Yi,Yuanfa Li,Kang Zhao,Jian Liang,Yuxuan Chen,Jinyuan Chen,Heng Qu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mi-Memory，一个面向个人AI的全生命周期记忆框架，围绕结构化、扩展、演化与部署四个角色组织，通过审计契约和证据驱动力求实现可审计、可治理、可部署的记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI正从纯聊天交互转向跨越手机、汽车、家居、可穿戴等多设备的持续服务。在此场景下，记忆不能仅是先前对话的缓存，而应作为连续性与治理基座：保持持久用户状态、基于多模态和设备证据回答问题、支持修正与遗忘、约束策略演化，并在延迟、成本、隐私和边缘-云约束下可部署。现有记忆系统缺乏统一的生命周期管理与可审计性。

Method: Mi-Memory框架围绕四个角色组织：Structure（通过MemStack实现记忆结构化）、Expansion（通过MemSense/MemFuse实现多模态感知与融合）、Evolution（通过D²ACCI/E²MEND实现策略演化与修正）、Deployment（通过LiteMem实现轻量化部署）。四个角色通过共享的审计契约链接，契约包含四类工件族：类型化证据载荷（保存来源身份与溯源）、诊断追踪（定位管道中的证据丢失）、策略工件（使记忆策略变更显式化）、门控/回滚记录（约束可接受的演化边界）。

Result: 在受控引用的结构化评估中，MemStack在LoCoMo、PersonaMem-V2和LongMemEval上分别达到93.59%、57.24%和87.47%；其他轨道报告模块级、初步/内部、迁移可行性或仅设计层面的证据并明确标注了边界。

Conclusion: Mi-Memory是迈向可审计、证据门控、部署感知的个人AI记忆系统的一步。该项目已开源并提供主页。

Abstract: Personal AI is moving beyond chat-only interaction toward continuous services that span phones, cars, homes, wearables, cameras, and tools. In this setting, memory cannot remain a cache of prior conversations. It should serve as a continuity and governance substrate: preserving durable user state, grounding answers in multimodal and device evidence, supporting correction and forgetting, bounding policy evolution, and remaining deployable under latency, cost, privacy, and edge-cloud constraints. This technical report presents Mi-Memory, a lifecycle memory framework for Personal AI organized around four roles: Structure, Expansion, Evolution, and Deployment. A shared audit contract links these roles through four recurring artifact families: typed evidence payloads preserve source identity and provenance, diagnostic traces localize evidence loss across the serving pipeline, strategy artifacts make memory-policy changes explicit, and gate/rollback records bound accepted evolution. MiMemory instantiates the roles through MemStack, MemSense/MemFuse, D$^{2}$ACCI/E$^{2}$MEND, and LiteMem. In controlled-reference Structure evaluations, MemStack reaches 93.59%, 57.24%, and 87.47% on LoCoMo, PersonaMem-V2, and LongMemEval, respectively; other tracks report module-level, preliminary/internal, transfer-feasibility, or design-only evidence with explicit boundaries. MiMemory is a step toward auditable, evidence-gated, and deployment-aware memory systems for Personal AI. Project homepage: https://darwin-agent.github.io/Mi-Memory/ .

</details>


### [106] [Fishing Out Free Riders: Shapley-Based Reward Attribution for Parallel Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18979)
*Wentao Zhang,Haoyu Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Yuxuan Cheng,Yuhan Pan,Miao Li,Zhipeng Qiao,Tao Feng,Zhen Tao,Dengji Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Parallel Shapley框架，利用Shapley值对多路径推理中各路径进行细粒度贡献分配，通过生成式奖励模型和蒙特卡洛采样实现高效近似，在数学推理基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推理方法将各推理路径视为等价，使用结果级奖励分配统一奖励，导致冗余、误导性路径与有效路径获得相同奖励，产生模糊的学习信号和不稳定的训练。

Method: 将每条推理路径视为合作博弈中的玩家，利用Shapley值量化边际贡献；采用生成式奖励模型评估路径效用，结合蒙特卡洛采样实现高效近似计算。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Parallel Shapley优于现有基线方法，训练更稳定且可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效识别并惩罚"搭便车"的冗余路径，按贡献比例分配奖励，从而提升多路径推理性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at multi-step reasoning, yet current parallel reasoning approaches often fail to distinguish the contributions of individual reasoning paths. Many paths may be redundant, misleading, or even detrimental, but outcome-level rewards assign uniform reward, leading to ambiguous learning signals and unstable training. We propose Parallel Shapley, a reinforcement learning framework that attributes fine-grained, path-level contributions in multi-path reasoning. Treating each path as a player in a cooperative game, we leverage Shapley values to quantify marginal contributions, using a generative reward model to evaluate path utilities and Monte Carlo sampling for efficient approximation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Parallel Shapley outperforms existing baselines while providing more stable and interpretable training. Our framework effectively "fishes out the free riders," assigning reward proportionally and improving multi-path reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [107] [Athena-Brain Technical Report: An Efficient Robot Brain for General Intelligence and Embodied Interactio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18985)
*Jialian Li,Junhong Liu,Yuchen Cao,Weiran Guo,Jiaming Song,Xutao Wang,Yi Zhao,Jiangpin Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Athena-Brain-8B是一个8B参数的大语言模型，通过多阶段后训练（通用SFT、通用RL、具身专家训练、模型合并），在保持通用智能的同时获得强的高层次具身交互能力，生成简洁响应，在通用和具身基准上均表现优异，甚至超越多个更大规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身智能体的发展，需要既保持LLM的通用广泛智能、又能有效与具身环境进行高层次交互的紧凑型端侧模型，但现有方法难以同时满足这两个需求。

Method: 多阶段后训练流程：通用监督微调（通用SFT）→ 通用强化学习（通用RL）→ 具身专家训练 → 模型合并（Model Merge）。

Result: 与Qwen3-8B思考模型相比，Athena-Brain-8B在通用语言与推理基准上表现相当，但响应更简洁；在领域内具身基准上，持续优于同规模模型，并零样本超越多个更大规模的顶尖模型。

Conclusion: 紧凑型语言模型能够有效整合强通用智能与具身能力，证明了小模型作为端侧具身大脑的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in language understanding, reasoning, and world knowledge. As embodied agents become increasingly capable, there is a growing demand for compact models that can serve as an on-device brain, preserving the broad general intelligence of LLMs while enabling effective high-level interaction with embodied environments. Existing approaches, however, often prioritize either general-purpose intelligence or specialized embodied capabilities, making it challenging to satisfy both requirements within a single model. We present \textbf{Athena-Brain-8B}, an 8B LLM designed to serve as an on-device brain for embodied intelligence for embodied intelligence. Through a multi-stage post-training pipeline consisting of General Supervised Fine-Tuning, General Reinforcement Learning, Embodied Expert training, and Model Merge, Athena-Brain-8B maintains strong general capabilities while acquiring strong high-level embodied interaction capabilities and generating concise responses for efficient embodied interaction. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of Athena across both general and embodied evaluations. Compared with the corresponding Qwen3-8B thinking model, Athena-Brain-8B achieves comparable performance on general language and reasoning benchmarks while generating substantially shorter responses. On in-domain embodied benchmarks, Athena-Brain-8B consistently outperforms models of similar scale and surpasses several substantially larger frontier models evaluated zero-shot, demonstrating that compact language models can effectively integrate strong general intelligence with embodied capabilities.

</details>


### [108] [Vector-Bench: Can Models Surgically Edit SVG Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19056)
*Yug Aditi Gupta,Prannay Hebbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vector-Bench是一个包含40个SVG修复任务的基准测试，用于评估指令式向量编辑能力。最强模型端点仅达到15.0%的完整规范成功率，尽管平均修复进展为43.7%，表明表面修复进展与忠实于规范的编辑之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式仅将输出作为光栅图像判断，容易忽略"保持未请求部分不变"这一关键能力，缺乏专门针对向量编辑的精细评估基准。

Method: 构建40个SVG修复任务，每个任务包含损坏的SVG程序、视觉指令、隐藏目标程序、平均5.05个带注释的修复和平均60.55个受保护对象。定义确定性二元规范奖励函数，结合属性感知感知容差和语义不变性要求，并引入有效性门控修复进度、接近完整层级和有效输出非预期变化率等诊断指标。

Result: 评估34个模型端点（1360个请求），最强端点仅达到15.0%完整规范成功率，平均修复进展43.7%，有效输出非预期变化率表明模型在保持未请求结构不变方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 现有的指令式向量编辑模型在忠实于规范的编辑方面仍有巨大提升空间，表面修复进展与规范忠实性之间存在本质差异，Vector-Bench可作为该领域的重要评估工具。

Abstract: Instruction-based vector editing requires two capabilities: making a requested change and leaving everything else alone. The second is easy to miss when an output is judged only as a raster image. We introduce Vector-Bench, a compact, difficult benchmark of 40 SVG repair tasks. Each task pairs a corrupted SVG program with an author-written visual instruction, a hidden target program, 5.05 annotated repairs on average, and an average of 60.55 protected objects. Instructions describe visible defects without exposing element identifiers, coordinates, color codes, or path data. We define a deterministic binary specification reward: requested repairs use attribute-aware perceptual tolerances, while unrequested rendering- or application-relevant structure must remain semantically unchanged and the result must be a valid SVG. Canonical target equality and stricter source fidelity are retained as diagnostics. Validity-gated repair progress, a near-complete tier, and valid-output Unintended Change Rate (UCR) explain partial outcomes. We evaluate 34 model endpoints (25 listed as open-weight, 5 inexpensive controls, and 4 frontier closed endpoints) over 1360 requests. The strongest endpoint reaches only 15.0% full specification success, despite 43.7% mean repair progress, showing that apparent repair progress and specification-faithful editing remain substantially different. All prompts, outputs, scoring code, costs, and per-task reports are released.

</details>


### [109] [Quality Action Assurance: Multimodal Verification of Examiner Claims in VR OSCEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19063)
*Harry Rogers,Sally Shiels,Ashley Tomlinson,James Thomas,James Aylward,Nathan Gauge,Helen Higham,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QAA（质量行动保证）多模态框架，用于在VR儿科OSCE中考官声明的客观验证。该框架通过约束时间对齐模型与LLM结合，实现93.4%时间对齐和79.2%事实正确性，显著提升考试评分的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSCE作为临床能力评估的金标准，其评分仍受考官主观性、疲劳和认知偏差影响。现行考官验证方法（如评分者间信度统计）缺乏对错误来源的解释力，无法分析考官推理过程或验证其声明与真实事件的一致性。

Method: QAA框架包含两个核心组件：（1）约束时间动作对齐模型，用于动作定位和参与者来源归因；（2）大语言模型，用于提取考官声明并与真实记录进行比对。数据来源包括视频、VR日志和演员数据。

Result: 5折交叉验证中，QAA达到99.2%±0.7%的Actor F1分数和93.4%±1.9%的W@16时间对齐精度。考官错误检测的精确率为70.0%，召回率为76.7%，事实正确性从39.2%提升至79.2%。

Conclusion: QAA框架能有效检测考官评分错误，提升OSCE评估的事实正确性和公平性，为临床能力评估提供客观验证手段。

Abstract: Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs) are the gold standard for assessing clinical competence, yet scoring remains vulnerable to examiner subjectivity, fatigue, and cognitive bias. Standard examiner validation via inter-rater statistics lacks explanatory power regarding the source of errors, as it neither analyzes examiner reasoning nor verifies examiner claims against actual events. Thus, we introduce Quality Action Assurance (QAA), a multimodal framework that verifies examiner claims in Virtual Reality (VR) pediatric OSCEs by comparing actions claimed by examiners against the true sequence of events, constructed from video, VR logs, and actor data. QAA combines a constrained temporal action alignment model, which performs action localization and actor source attribution, with a large language model that extracts examiner claims and checks them against the record. Across a 5-fold cross-validation, QAA achieves 99.2% $\pm$ 0.7% Actor F1 and 93.4% $\pm$ 1.9% W@16 for temporal alignment. Overall, QAA detects examiner errors with 70.0% precision and 76.7% recall, improving factual correctness from 39.2% to 79.2%, enabling fairer OSCE assessment.

</details>


### [110] [On the Effectiveness of Pretraining for Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19072)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对图组合优化问题的自监督预训练框架，利用几何增广（旋转和轴向反射）的图对比学习，使模型学习不变的结构表示和全局相对距离分布，在TSP等路由问题上显著优于非预训练模型，混合策略在TSP1000上实现了6.57%的路径长度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经求解器在路由问题（如TSP）上缺乏有效的预训练策略，难以扩展到高维实例，需要引入能够捕捉问题几何结构的归纳偏置来提升模型的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出自监督预训练框架，采用图对比学习结合几何增广（旋转和轴向反射），迫使模型学习对几何变换不变的拓扑结构表示以及全局相对距离分布，然后在下游任务中微调。

Result: 在各种问题规模上均优于非预训练模型，其中混合策略（旋转+反射）在TSP1000上实现了6.57%的路径长度改进。

Conclusion: 几何预训练是有效扩展神经求解器到高维实例的重要归纳偏置。

Abstract: This paper introduces a self-supervised pretraining framework for graph combinatorial optimization specifically designed to address the nature of routing problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem. By utilizing graph contrastive learning with geometric augmentations (specifically, rotations and axial reflections) the model is forced to learn invariant structural representations and global relative distance distributions. Results demonstrate that this pretraining strategy outperforms non-pretrained models across various problem scales. Notably, the hybrid strategy (combining rotation and reflection) achieved a 6.57% improvement in tour length for TSP1000, proving that geometric pretraining is an important inductive bias for effectively scaling neural solvers to high-dimensional instances.

</details>


### [111] [Supra Cognitive Modes: A Routed Architecture for Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19096)
*Joshua Tobkin,David Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM)架构，通过语义分类器和运行时门控将查询路由至不同检索与合成模式，在共享存储基底上实现高效处理。在LoCoMo、MAB和LongMemEval三个基准上评估了部署配置，分别取得84.87%、61.49%和86.00%的成绩，并通过仓库可复现实验支持故障分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent记忆工作负载混合了直接事实查找、关系链与当前状态推理以及长历史综合等多种查询类型，现有单一检索模式难以同时高效处理这些异构查询，因此需要一种能够根据查询类型动态路由到不同处理模式的架构。

Method: 设计SCM架构，包含：1) 冻结的语义分类器，将查询映射到显式或自动选择的认知模式；2) 运行时门控机制，将查询分发至词法+密集查找、图或多跳迭代推理、分层长形式综合三种模式；3) 共享存储基底，融合多粒度嵌入、抽取三元组、事实版本元数据和异步增强信息。

Result: 在LoCoMo上事实类准确率84.87%、对抗性弃权68.61%；MAB两次重复平均61.49%；LongMemEval达86.00%。仓库复现获得相似聚合分数，支持任务和模式条件化故障分析。但缺少原始基线输出、端到端时序和完整token账目，因果路由效应、效率提升和统计显著性未经验证。

Conclusion: SCM作为一种路由式多模式检索架构在三个记忆基准上展示了可行性和诊断性故障模式，但因果路由效果、效率增益和统计显著性等关键优势尚未得到充分证据支持。

Abstract: Agent-memory workloads mix direct factual lookup, relation-chain and current-state reasoning, and broad synthesis over long histories. We describe Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM), an architecture that maps explicit or automatically selected per-query modes to retrieval and synthesis payloads over one shared ingest substrate. A frozen semantic classifier and runtime gates dispatch queries among fused lexical and dense lookup, graph or iterative multi-hop handling, and stratified long-form synthesis. The substrate combines multi-granularity embeddings, extracted triples, fact-version metadata, and optional asynchronous enrichments. We characterize the deployed configuration on three benchmarks: Long-term Conversational Memory (LoCoMo; n = 1,986), MemoryAgentBench (MAB; n = 3,671), and LongMemEval (n = 500). The reference run records 84.87% on LoCoMo factoid categories and 68.61% on adversarial abstention, 61.49% on MAB across two repetitions, and 86.00% on LongMemEval. A repository-backed reproduction produces similar aggregate scores and supports task- and mode-conditioned failure analysis. Raw baseline outputs, aligned end-to-end timing for LoCoMo and LongMemEval, and complete token ledgers are unavailable; stored rows also omit some final runtime decisions. The results characterize one implemented routed configuration and its diagnostic failure patterns, while source inspection verifies the per-query control interface and shared-substrate design. Causal routing effects, efficiency gains, and statistical significance remain outside the available evidence.

</details>


### [112] [OpenRTAG: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robust Text-Attributed Graph Learning under Data Quality Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19108)
*Yuze Dai,Zhihan Zhang,Yan Zhao,Ruoyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Qiangqiang Dai,Hongchao Qin,Ronghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRTAG基准，系统研究文本属性图在文本、结构和标签三个维度的质量退化（稀疏、噪声、不平衡）对图学习的影响，通过统一的3×3分类法和9个数据集、3个任务，评估传统GNN、LLM-GNN和图基础模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的文本属性图常存在文本、结构和标签的质量问题（稀疏、噪声、不平衡），但现有研究分散在不同退化类型、数据集、任务和模型上，缺乏统一的鲁棒性评估框架。

Method: 构建了3×3分类法（文本/结构/标签 × 稀疏/噪声/不平衡）定义9种退化场景，在9个TAG数据集和3个下游任务上系统评估传统GNN、LLM-GNN和图基础模型，包括场景有效性验证、模型敏感性分析、基线方法对比以及复合退化场景下的行为分析。

Result: OpenRTAG提供了标准化的测试平台，系统揭示了不同退化场景下各类模型的鲁棒性表现，包括模型敏感性差异、基线方法的有效性/效率/鲁棒性权衡，以及复合退化场景下的模型行为规律。

Conclusion: OpenRTAG填补了文本属性图学习鲁棒性系统性评估的空白，为低质量真实场景下的TAG学习提供了标准化基准，有助于推动该领域的系统研究。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) are an important graph data form that combine relational structure with rich node text. However, real-world TAGs are often imperfect, with quality issues arising from text, structure, and labels, and typically manifesting as sparsity, noise, and imbalance. These dimensions define nine representative degradation scenarios that can substantially affect TAG learning. Although prior studies have explored specific mitigation strategies, existing evidence remains fragmented across degradation types, datasets, tasks, and model families, leaving TAG robustness insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we present OpenRTAG, a robustness benchmark for text-attributed graph learning. OpenRTAG organizes TAG quality issues into a unified 3 * 3 taxonomy and supports standardized evaluation across nine TAG datasets and three downstream tasks. It systematically evaluates scenario validity and model sensitivity, compares traditional GNNs, LLM-GNNs, and a representative GFM, investigates the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of scenario-matched baselines, and further examines model behavior under composite degradation scenarios. OpenRTAG provides a standardized testbed for understanding robustness in TAG learning under realistic low-quality settings.

</details>


### [113] [Comparative Study of Multi-Agent Actor-Critic Algorithms in Parameterized Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19117)
*Ubayd Ali Bapoo,Clement N Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出共享经验的多智能体扩展算法（MAGAC、MASAC、MATQC），用于参数化动作强化学习。实验表明该框架对GAC性能提升显著，但对SAC和TQC提升有限；智能体数量超过5个后计算成本大幅增加而收益递减，揭示了学习性能与计算效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作强化学习在单智能体场景中已取得良好效果（如GAC、SAC、TQC），但其在多智能体场景中的扩展性尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究如何将这些算法有效推广到多智能体环境。

Method: 采用共享经验的多智能体框架：不使用CTDE范式，而是让多个独立Actor-Critic智能体共享回放缓冲区（replay buffer），同时保持各自的策略网络和价值网络。在Platform-v0和Goal-v0基准上，分别用3、5、10个智能体配置进行实验，与单智能体版本对比，使用平均评估回报和训练时间衡量性能，并采用单因素方差分析和Tukey HSD事后检验进行统计显著性评估。

Result: 共享经验多智能体框架显著提升了GAC的性能，但对SAC和TQC的提升较为温和。增加智能体数量超过5个时，性能提升有限，而计算成本（尤其是MAGAC）大幅增加。

Conclusion: 共享经验的多智能体Actor-Critic方法在参数化动作强化学习中存在学习性能与计算效率之间的权衡，为未来该方向的扩展提供了可扩展性方面的见解。

Abstract: Parameterized action reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in environments requiring both discrete action selection and continuous parameterization. Prior work established the effectiveness of single-agent actor-critic algorithms - Greedy Actor-Critic (GAC), Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), and Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC) - on benchmark parameterized action tasks, but their extension to multi-agent settings remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a comparative study of shared-experience multi-agent extensions of these algorithms: Multi-Agent Greedy Actor-Critic (MAGAC), Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC), and Multi-Agent Truncated Quantile Critics (MATQC). Rather than following the centralized training, decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm, the proposed framework uses multiple independent actor-critic agents that share a replay buffer while maintaining separate policy and value networks. We evaluate the algorithms on the Platform-v0 and Goal-v0 benchmarks against their single-agent counterparts, using three-, five-, and ten-agent configurations to assess scalability. Performance is measured by average evaluation return and training time across ten independent runs, with one-way ANOVA and Tukey HSD post-hoc tests used to assess statistical significance. Results show that the multi-agent framework consistently improves Greedy Actor-Critic performance, while MASAC and MATQC show comparatively modest gains over their single-agent versions. Increasing the number of agents beyond five yields limited additional performance while substantially raising computational cost, particularly for MAGAC. These results highlight a trade-off between learning performance and computational efficiency, offering insight into the scalability of shared-experience multi-agent actor-critic methods for parameterized action reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [114] [Sequential Learner Modeling Using Multi-Relational Graph Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19253)
*Rawaa Alatrash,Mohamed Amine Chatti,Hong Yang,Yumeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-ConceptGCN，一种基于多关系图卷积网络和预训练语言模型的无监督概念级序列学习者建模方法，通过结合个人知识图谱、MR-GCN和SBERT增强语义表示，并在在线用户研究中验证了其在教育推荐系统中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN用户建模方法存在两个主要不足：一是将图中不同关系类型视为同质的，未能捕捉丰富语义；二是忽略了用户交互序列信息。目前多关系GNN在用户建模领域尚未被探索。

Method: MR-ConceptGCN融合了个人知识图谱(PKG)、多关系图卷积网络(MR-GCN)和预训练语言模型SBERT，获得增强的关系感知和语义感知的PKG项表示。利用学习者互动中未理解的知识概念的增强嵌入，构建结合长期和短期交互的序列学习者模型。

Result: 通过31名参与者的在线用户研究，MR-ConceptGCN在教育推荐系统的准确性、有用性、多样性和满意度等关键用户中心指标上表现出优势。

Conclusion: 多关系GCN结合预训练语言模型能有效增强用户建模中的语义表示，且序列化建模长短期交互对个性化推荐有正面作用。

Abstract: User modeling is a critical task in a variety of personalized systems. Recognizing their effectiveness in learning from graph-structured data, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), particularly Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), are increasingly employed for user modeling. However, existing approaches typically treat different relation types in a graph as homogeneous, limiting their ability to capture richer semantics and construct more informative user models. While multi-relational GNNs (MR-GNNs) have been adopted for representation learning and recommendation, their application for user modeling remains unexplored. Moreover, existing GNN-based user modeling approaches ignore the user interaction sequence. To address these research gaps, in this work we propose MR-ConceptGCN, a novel fully unsupervised approach focused on concept-based sequential learner modeling using multi-relational GCNs (MR-GCNs). MR-ConceptGCN effecively combines Personal Knowledge Graphs (PKGs), MR-GCNs, and the pre-trained language model SBERT to obtain enhanced relation- and semantic-aware representations of the PKG items. The enriched embeddings of the knowledge concepts that a learner did not understand when interacting with learning materials in CourseMapper are then used to construct a sequential learner model that combines long-term and short-term learner interactions. We report the results of an online user study (n = 31), demonstrating the benefits of MR-ConceptGCN in terms of several important user-centric aspects including accuracy, usefulness, diversity, and satisfaction with an educational recommender system.

</details>


### [115] [BioSecBench-Surveillance: A Verifiable Benchmark for AI Agents in Pathogen Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19262)
*Harmon Bhasin,Kevin Flyangolts,Dianzhuo Wang,Evan Seeyave,Arjun Banerjee,Amanda Darling,Joshua Stallings,David Stern,Shawn Higdon,Claire Duvallet,Bryan Tegomoh,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioSecBench-Surveillance基准测试，包含100个评估任务，用于测试AI代理能否从原始测序数据和监测背景中推断出正确的分析流程。最强的模型配置（Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex）仅达到约50%的准确率，表明AI在基因组监测分析方面仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着病原体基因组监测规模的扩大，瓶颈正从数据生成转向数据分析。现有的AI代理缺乏标准化基准来评估其能否像人类分析师一样，仅从原始测序数据和监测背景中自主推断并执行正确的分析流程。

Method: 构建了包含100个评估任务的BioSecBench-Surveillance基准，涵盖7个类别（从分类学分类到基因工程检测），涉及多种样本类型和测序技术。每个评估仅提供给AI代理与人类分析师相同的数据和背景信息，然后以确定性方式对结构化答案进行评分。

Result: 在来自16个模型-工具组合的3,962次可评分尝试中，最强配置（Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex）均仅达到50.2%的正确率。即使代理调用了正确的工作流，错误仍来自参数选择（参考数据库、阈值、过滤器、归一化方式等）。

Conclusion: BioSecBench-Surveillance为衡量AI代理是否值得信任以执行基因组监测任务提供了标准化基准。当前最先进的AI代理在该任务上性能有限，尚不具备独立可靠地执行基因组监测分析的能力。

Abstract: As pathogen genomic surveillance scales, the bottleneck is shifting from data generation to analysis. We present BioSecBench-Surveillance, a verifiable benchmark of 100 evaluations testing whether AI agents can infer the right analysis pipeline from raw sequencing data and surveillance context. Each evaluation gives an agent only the data and context a human analyst would have, then grades its structured answer deterministically. The tasks span seven categories, from taxonomic classification to genetic-engineering detection, across diverse sample types and sequencing technologies. Across 3,962 gradable attempts from sixteen model-harness pairs, the strongest configuration cleared only about half. Opus 4.8 with PI led at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.1 to 60.3 percent across 83 evaluations, tied with GPT-5.5 with Codex at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.8 to 59.6 percent, followed by Opus 4.7 with PI at 49.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.0 to 59.2 percent, and Sonnet 4.6 with PI at 48.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 38.9 to 58.3 percent. Even when agents invoked the correct workflows, their mistakes came from the choices around them, such as which references, thresholds, filters, and normalization to apply. BioSecBench-Surveillance provides a standard for measuring whether agents can be trusted to perform genomic surveillance when the next outbreak arrives.

</details>


### [116] [Graph-Based Agentic AI with LangGraph: Workflow Pathways for Long-Running Stateful Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19297)
*Daniel Pearson,Sidney Shapiro,Emiliano Sebastian Gonzalez Venegas,Sanad Al-Khatib,Aurora Pinzón Arzola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提供了面向长期运行、有状态、多步骤生成式AI系统的图式工作流实践指南，给出三个可执行的LangGraph配方（SQL分析带修复循环、带证据门控的智能体RAG、带中断与检查点恢复的人工审核流程），并说明LangGraph适用于复杂工作流，而简单场景应选用ReAct/SDK或DSPy等替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务过程中的长期运行、有状态、多步骤生成式AI系统需要结构化的工作流编排，但现有实践往往将LangGraph视为模型质量的基准目标，而非根据工作流复杂度选择合适工具的编排框架，缺乏针对何时以及如何使用图式工作流的实践指导。

Method: 以三个可执行配方（SQL分析修复循环、智能体RAG证据门控、人工审核中断恢复）展示LangGraph中类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试、中断、检查点和追踪的整合方式，并通过与ReAct/SDK、Schema优先工具、DSPy的对比，明确LangGraph的适用边界。

Result: 给出了三个具体的可执行工作流配方，明确了LangGraph在复杂工作流中的价值（路由、暂停、审计轨迹显式化），同时指出简单工具调用应使用ReAct/SDK，结构化提取验证应使用Schema优先工具，提示/程序优化应使用DSPy。

Conclusion: LangGraph应根据工作流复杂度选择使用，而非作为通用默认方案；在需要显式路由、暂停和审计轨迹的场景下，LangGraph的额外结构是值得的。

Abstract: This paper is a practitioner guide to graph-based workflow pathways for long-running, stateful, multi-step generative AI systems in business processes. Rather than treating LangGraph, a low-level orchestration framework for stateful agents, as a model-quality benchmark target, we present three executable recipes -- SQL analytics with repair loops, agentic retrieval-augmented generation with evidence gating, and human-in-the-loop policy review with interrupt and checkpoint recovery -- to show how typed state, conditional routing, deterministic tools, retries, interrupts, checkpoints, and traces fit together. LangGraph is positioned by workflow-complexity fit, not as a universal default: simpler ReAct-style or plain SDK loops may be better for basic tool use, schema-first tools for structured extraction and validation, and DSPy when prompt or program optimization is the main goal. Each recipe explains when LangGraph is worth the extra structure and which implementation patterns make routes, pauses, and audit trails explicit product behavior rather than hidden prompt logic.

</details>


### [117] [LLM Detection as an Intervention: Downstream Impact under Strategic User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19300)
*Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Jon Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完美的LLM检测器如何通过改变用户使用LLM的激励方式，反直觉地导致LLM使用量增加和输出质量下降，并揭示了检测器对检测属性呈现"先升后降"的清洁模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛采用，检测LLM生成内容的需求日益增长，但现有检测器作为干预手段会对下游指标（如LLM使用量和输出质量）产生非预期影响，本文旨在揭示这些反直觉效应。

Method: 构建了一个风格化模型，刻画用户策略性地选择LLM使用程度以及对内容进行后处理以降低被检测属性的行为，并在arXiv摘要的词频数据上进行实证验证。

Result: 1) LLM检测可能反直觉地导致人类增加LLM使用量；2) 即使降低检测属性有助于提升输出质量，引入检测器仍可能导致用户产出更低质量的内容；3) 检测器对检测属性呈现清晰的"先升后降"模式。

Conclusion: LLM检测会扭曲LLM的使用和输出质量，揭示了当检测器作为下游指标干预手段运作时存在的失效模式。

Abstract: As LLM adoption becomes more widespread, there is a growing interest in detecting LLM-generated content, for example through LLM detection tools and through heuristics based on language patterns. Detectors operate as an intervention that steers not only the detected attribute itself, but also downstream metrics such as LLM usage and output quality. In this work, we demonstrate how imperfect LLM detectors lead to counterintuitive impacts on these downstream metrics, by distorting how users are incentivized to use LLMs in their workflow. We develop a stylized model which captures how users strategically choose how much to use the LLM and how to post-process content to reduce the detected attribute. Using this model, we show that LLM detection can counterintuitively lead humans to increase their LLM usage. Moreover, even when reducing the detected attribute improves output quality, we find that introducing an LLM detector can lead users to produce lower quality outputs. In contrast, we show that detectors result in a clean "rise-then-fall" pattern for the detected attribute, which we empirically reproduce for word frequencies on arXiv abstracts. Altogether, our work illustrates how LLM detection can distort LLM usage and output quality, uncovering failure modes when LLM detectors operate as an intervention on these downstream metrics.

</details>


### [118] [ResearchArena: Evaluating Sabotage and Monitoring in Automated AI R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19321)
*Lena Libon,Ben Rank,Jehyeok Yeon,David Schmotz,Jeremy Qin,Daniel Donnelly,Derck Prinzhorn,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI控制方法，通过将AI代理视为潜在 adversary 并利用监控器检测隐蔽破坏，来评估自动化AI研发产出的安全性。在ResearchArena框架（涵盖安全后训练、能力后训练、CUDA内核优化和推理服务器优化四项长周期任务）上评估了前沿代理的破坏与监控能力，发现隐藏在训练数据中的破坏最难捕获（不到一半被标记），仅允许监控器运行实验仍不足够。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理开始自动化AI研发，需要可靠方法来评估其产出是否安全部署，尤其是在代理本身可能不可信的情况下。传统信任代理的方法存在风险，因此需要一种不依赖代理可信度的替代方案。

Method: 提出AI控制方法，将代理视为潜在 adversary并部署监控器检测隐蔽破坏；构建ResearchArena框架，包含四项长周期AI研发任务，每项任务配有两种隐蔽侧任务（嵌入式破坏和独立侧任务）；评估四种监控器类型（沿两个维度变化：是否可执行和探查产物、是否查看代理思维链）。

Result: 隐藏在训练数据中的破坏最难捕获，标记率不到一半；允许监控器运行实验虽有帮助但不足够，监控器仍因仅检查表面、注意到异常但错误解释、或用错误测试探查而遗漏嵌入式破坏。

Conclusion: AI控制是评估自动化AI研发安全的可行方法，但现有监控能力仍有显著局限，尤其是对训练数据隐藏的破坏检测不足。ResearchArena作为模块化框架发布，可支持未来对AI研发中破坏与控制的评估研究。

Abstract: As AI agents begin to automate AI R&D, we need ways to assess whether their outputs are safe to deploy, even when the agents themselves may be untrusted. AI control offers one such approach: rather than trusting the agent, it treats it as a potential adversary and uses a monitor to detect covert sabotage before deployment. We evaluate AI control for automated AI R&D with ResearchArena, a framework spanning four long-horizon tasks: safety post-training, capabilities post-training, CUDA-kernel optimization, and inference-server optimization. Because the deliverable in AI R&D is an artifact that will be deployed and run, we pair each main task with two kinds of hidden side task: an embedded side task that sabotages the submitted model, adapter, kernel, or server itself, and an independent side task that takes a covert out-of-scope action in the sandbox while leaving the artifact honest. We evaluate frontier agents at both sabotage and monitoring, across four monitor types that vary along two axes: whether the monitor may execute and probe the produced artifact, and whether it sees the agent's chain-of-thought. We find that sabotage hidden in the training data is the hardest to catch, flagged fewer than half the time. Letting the monitor run experiments on the artifact, rather than only read the trajectory, helps, but it is not enough: monitors still miss embedded sabotage by inspecting only the surface, by noticing the anomaly but explaining it away, or by probing the artifact with the wrong test. We release ResearchArena as a modular framework for evaluating sabotage and control in automated AI R&D.

</details>


### [119] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个深度神经网络模型，用于模拟视觉效价加工中的联想情绪学习，该模型包含视觉编码模块和情绪效价识别模块。通过在新型巴甫洛夫学习范式上的测试，模型再现了人类联想学习的多个现象（包括联结形成和泛化），并发现条件刺激与非条件刺激的神经表征在单个单元和群体水平上逐渐对齐。与人类实验数据的对比进一步验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如Rescorla-Wagner模型）在解释联想情绪学习方面存在局限，尤其在应用于神经数据时。深度神经网络的兴起为建模这一重要功能提供了新的可能性，但相关研究尚不充分。

Method: 构建了一个包含视觉模块（编码复杂自然场景）和情绪效价识别模块的深度神经网络模型，并在新型巴甫洛夫学习范式上进行了测试。

Result: 模型成功再现了人类联想学习中的联结形成和泛化现象；条件刺激与非条件刺激的神经表征在单单元和群体水平上逐渐对齐；与人类实验数据的比较验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络模型结合适当的学习算法，可用于建模联想情绪/效价学习的行为和神经特征。

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [120] [Agents in the Wild: Where Research Meets Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19336)
*Grace Hui Yang,Pranav N. Venkit,Hooman Sedghamiz,Enrico Santus,Victor Dibia,Ioana Baldini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于基于大型语言模型的智能体系统（Agentic Systems）的教程综述，聚焦于从研究原型到生产部署过程中的鲁棒性、安全性与可靠性挑战，涵盖推理规划、多智能体协调、评估等关键议题，并通过制药发现和金融系统等案例提供了设计模式与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学术界已在基准测试和算法创新上取得进展，但智能体系统在实际生产部署中面临鲁棒性、安全性与可靠性的新挑战，现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 通过教程形式汇集研究人员与实践者，分析推理与规划、多智能体协调、评估等方面的进展，结合制药发现与金融系统的应用案例，总结成功设计模式及失败缓解策略（如验证流水线、回退机制、人在回环监督）。

Result: 给出了智能体系统成功部署的常见设计模式、评估检查清单以及跨行业安全可靠部署的模板。

Conclusion: 参会者将获得该领域的全面视角，以及适用于各行业安全可靠部署的具体设计模式、评估清单和模板。

Abstract: Agentic systems large language model (LLM) based architectures capable of reasoning, planning, acting, and coordinating with tools and other agents are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to production scale deployments across domains such as software engineering, scientific discovery, and finance. While academic work has emphasized benchmarks and algorithmic innovation, deployment raises new challenges around robustness, safety, and reliability. This tutorial brings together researchers and practitioners to explore advances in reasoning and planning, multi agent coordination, and evaluation, highlighting open challenges arising from deployment experience. Through applied case studies in pharmaceutical discovery and financial systems, we analyze common design patterns that make agentic systems successful, and discuss practical mitigation strategies for failure modes, such as verification pipelines, fallback mechanisms, and human in the loop supervision. Attendees will gain a comprehensive view of the field along with concrete design patterns, evaluation checklists, and templates for safe and reliable deployment across industries.

</details>


### [121] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向编码智能体的预算控制框架，在智能体执行失败后，利用执行反馈决定是进行廉价恢复还是升级到更强模型。通过训练监督式路由器和添加保形风险控制层，在保证解决率的同时显著降低平均恢复成本（在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设定下，使用35%的平均恢复成本达到甚至超过始终升级的解决率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本感知系统通常将失败视为级联决策（先用廉价模型，失败后升级到昂贵模型），但在编码任务中，执行反馈可能使廉价模型的进一步恢复尝试仍然有价值。因此需要回答一个预算部署问题：何时应该花更多廉价计算，何时应该升级到更强模型。

Method: 将失败后的决策建模为异构动作上的恢复路由（recovery routing），从执行轨迹中训练一个监督式路由器；引入保形风险控制层，无需重新训练即可在部署时选择成本惩罚项，并在可交换性假设下提供边际期望成本控制。

Result: 在五个编码基准的保留失败样本上，廉价恢复和升级表现出互补的成功模式。校准后的前沿优于固定动作、仅提示路由器和二元级联基线；在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设定下，一个CRC校准的前沿点以35%的平均恢复成本达到了超过始终升级的解决率。

Conclusion: 提出的预算控制框架能有效平衡编码智能体的解决率和成本，保形风险控制层使路由器在不同预算下均能灵活部署。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Formulation-Level Auto-Tuning for QUBO-Based Machine Learning: A Case Study Across Multiple Quantum-Inspired Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18774)
*Naoya Mizuki,Takahiro Katagiri,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Optuna的自动调优框架，用于在多个量子启发式退火器上实现支持向量机（SVM）的公式级参数联合优化，实验表明该方法在含噪分类任务上优于传统网格搜索约0.8~2.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于退火的SVM中，连续对偶变量需离散化为QUBO模型，引入了表示参数（编码基B和比特深度K）、RBF核参数γ和等式约束惩罚ξ三类耦合参数，其联合选择是一个复杂的混合离散-连续黑箱优化问题，传统网格搜索效率低且效果有限。

Method: 提出两级优化框架：内层退火器最小化生成的QUBO，外层Optuna循环在每次试验中重构公式并最大化验证准确率。使用TPE和高斯过程采样器，在Fixstars Amplify、Toshiba SQBM+和Fujitsu Digital Annealer三个退火器上执行，并与网格搜索对比。

Result: 在线性和非线性分类任务（0-20%标签噪声）上，平均比网格搜索提升约0.8个百分点（线性）和2.1个百分点（非线性）。

Conclusion: 公式质量与后端能力必须联合评估；任务级反馈可以补偿离散化、惩罚不平衡和后端依赖的近似优化问题。

Abstract: This paper presents an Optuna-based formulation-level auto-tuning framework for support vector machines (SVMs) implemented on multiple quantum-inspired annealers. In an annealing-based SVM, continuous dual variables are discretized and converted into a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. This transformation introduces three coupled classes of parameters: representation parameters-the encoding base B and bit depth K-which determine numerical range, resolution, and QUBO size; the RBF kernel parameter γ, which determines classifier geometry; and the equality-constraint penalty ξ, which controls feasibility and coefficient balance. We formulate their joint selection as a mixed discrete-continuous black-box optimization problem. The framework has two optimization levels: an inner annealer minimizes the generated QUBO, while an outer Optuna loop reconstructs the formulation in every trial and maximizes validation accuracy. The same solver-agnostic procedure is applied to Fixstars Amplify Annealing Engine, Toshiba SQBM+, and Fujitsu Digital Annealer using TPE and Gaussian-process samplers and is compared with conventional grid search. Experiments on linear and nonlinear classification tasks with 0-20% label noise show mean gains over grid search of approximately 0.8 and 2.1 percentage points, respectively. The results demonstrate that formulation quality and backend capability must be evaluated jointly and that task-level feedback can compensate for discretization, penalty imbalance, and backend-dependent approximate optimization.

</details>


### [123] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON-Discover框架，利用置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性等差异信号，对表格数据中高度自信但错误的预测（即错误自信集中现象）进行检测。实验表明，基于差异的排序方法在捕捉危险错误方面显著优于传统校准或信任评分基线，且最佳检测器因数据集而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准评估关注全局聚合指标，但最危险的失败往往是局部的——模型在错误预测时依然保持高置信度。这种错误自信集中的现象未被充分研究，亟需有效的检测方法。

Method: 提出FALCON-Discover，一种后验、模型无关的框架。它利用置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性产生的差异信号对预测进行排序，在七个二元表格数据集、四种随机种子、五折交叉拟合以及XGBoost和CatBoost等强学习器上进行评估。

Result: 错误自信集中现象普遍存在但具有条件依赖性。在主要置信阈值下，基于差异的排序在最强场景中显著优于验证集选择的校准或信任评分基线，而原始置信度几乎无法捕捉危险错误。最佳检测器因数据集而异：学习差异在需要组合多种线索时最优，稳定性排序在局部决策脆弱性主导时最佳。

Conclusion: 危险过度自信更适合被视为一个族级发现问题而非单一分数校准问题，应设计专门针对置信度、支持和稳定性存在分歧区域的校准策略。

Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略，通过结合输出置信度与频谱特征（如频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持和相位稳定性）来估计时间序列分类预测的可信度，在保持骨干预测不变的前提下提升选择性可靠性指标（Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.786，FalseConf@0.9降至0.094）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分类的后验校准方法存在三个可靠性缺口：相同置信度值可能掩盖不同的时间支撑证据、平均校准指标可能遗漏高置信度错误、输出空间重校准缺乏与输入信号关联的可审计性。

Method: 提出验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略，保持骨干预测不变，将输出侧置信度与全样本频谱描述符（频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持、相位稳定性）结合形成标量可靠性估计和诊断性频带证据。验证门控仅在正确性排序提升且不违反FalseConf@0.9或AURC容忍度时启用频谱条件化，否则回退到输出空间基线。

Result: 在8个UCR/UEA数据集、8种时间序列骨干网络和标准重校准器上，无约束方法将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779，验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，并将FalseConf@0.9降低至0.094。

Conclusion: 时间序列分类的可靠性估计应结合输出置信度与频谱证据，验证门控机制可防止无支撑的频谱条件化，提供更可靠的预测信任估计。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究结合静态参数剪枝和动态token跳层两种压缩机制的复合稀疏化方法，发现其在同等稀疏度下优于单一机制，能延迟性能退化点，并揭示存在跨维度稀疏性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算压缩在超过某个稀疏性边界后会引发快速性能退化，本文探索将两种机制结合是否能通过分摊压缩负担来延迟这种退化。

Method: 提出复合稀疏框架：先通过低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩骨干网络，再引入轻量级路由器实现逐token的动态层跳过，从而独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。

Result: 在语言理解和建模基准上，复合稀疏在同等总稀疏度下始终优于单一压缩机制，在理解任务上延迟了性能衰减点，并保持了更强的建模性能。近平衡的分配在固定稀疏预算下最有效。

Conclusion: 复合压缩是提升LLM压缩效果的实用方法，但也揭示了更广泛的跨维度稀疏性边界，最终限制了进一步的压缩。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [126] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALAS核函数族，通过对称α稳定谱成分构建灵活的高斯过程核，能够自适应学习数据的平滑度，在多样化的黑箱优化任务中表现出稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化的成功高度依赖于核函数选择是否匹配目标函数的未知结构，而传统核函数缺乏灵活性，难以同时捕捉平滑趋势和尖锐不规则性。

Method: 提出ALAS核函数族，基于对称α稳定谱成分构建高斯过程核。通过学习稳定性参数α自适应调整有效平滑度。提出两种参数化形式：ALAS（单平稳成分联合谱调制）和ALAS-Sep（可分离变体，按维度学习尾行为以增强对近似可分解目标的鲁棒性）。

Result: 在标准基准测试和真实世界代理任务上的实验表明，ALAS在多样化设置下表现出强大且稳健的性能。

Conclusion: ALAS通过自适应学习数据平滑度，有效解决了核函数选择难题，为贝叶斯优化提供了一种更灵活、鲁棒的核函数方案。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

</details>


### [127] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC，一种基于联邦学习的框架，用于胎儿超声图像中胼胝体的准确定位。该框架集成冻结的DINOv2主干网络与轻量级YOLO检测头，并通过LoRA模块实现参数高效微调。在包含10,970帧超声图像的多中心数据集上，FedCC在联邦设置下取得了平均mAP@50为0.857、F1-score为0.803的性能，同时将可训练参数从24.4M减少至2.9M，通信成本降低约8.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声图像中胼胝体的准确定位对于早期识别神经发育异常至关重要，但超声成像存在低对比度、散斑噪声以及胼胝体解剖变异大等固有挑战。现有方法难以在无需数据共享的多中心、资源受限的临床环境中有效部署。

Method: 提出FedCC框架，采用冻结的DINOv2作为视觉主干，结合轻量级YOLO检测头。通过引入Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)模块实现参数高效微调，仅优化和交换少量参数，降低计算与通信开销。采用FedAvg聚合策略进行联邦学习。

Result: 在多中心数据集（10,970帧，58名孕妇，3个临床站点）上，FedCC在联邦设置下达到平均mAP@50为0.857、F1-score为0.803。可训练参数从24.4M降至2.9M，通信成本降低约8.5倍，性能优于全微调与编码器冻结基线。

Conclusion: FedCC为可扩展、隐私保护且适合临床部署的胎儿神经超声AI系统迈出了有前景的一步，验证了联邦学习与参数高效微调在医学影像分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [128] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合参数重要性和逐层功能等价性的混合SVD压缩方法，并设计了一种计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，在高压缩比下性能优于或持平于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模庞大，内存需求巨大，限制了在资源受限环境中的应用。现有SVD压缩方法要么从参数重要性角度出发，要么从逐层功能等价性角度出发，但未将两者结合；同时压缩率分配多采用均匀分配或计算昂贵的启发式搜索，缺乏高效方案。

Method: 1) 将参数重要性和逐层功能等价性两种视角融合到单一目标函数中进行低秩近似压缩；2) 提出一种增强的、计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，替代均匀分配或昂贵搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在压缩性能上与现有最优方法持平或显著更优，尤其在高压缩比场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 融合两种压缩视角并采用高效动态分配策略，能在不增加计算负担的情况下有效提升SVD压缩质量，是一种实用且高效的NN压缩方案。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [129] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E-SpecFormer（边缘频谱监测Transformer），一种用于自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别的端到端模型。其核心创新是LiTAN（线性Tanh注意力网络），一种无需Softmax和LayerNorm的注意力机制，在降低复杂度的同时提升RF任务精度。模型提供四种可扩展变体（Nano, Small, Medium, Large）以适应不同硬件约束。在RadioML2018数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB时达到86.5%的平均准确率；在基于硬件木马的隐蔽信道数据集上达到94.2%准确率，参数量不足10k，FPGA/CPU协同执行速度达每帧92μs，以极低成本超越现有边缘模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别方法在边缘设备上计算开销大、推理速度慢，难以满足物联网设备的实时性要求。需要一种轻量级、高效率的端到端模型，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低参数量和推理延迟。

Method: 提出E-SpecFormer框架，核心组件为LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network），用Tanh激活函数替代Softmax归一化，并去除LayerNorm，简化注意力计算；提供四种参数量可扩展的变体（Nano, Small, Medium, Large）以适应不同边缘设备；采用FPGA/CPU协同执行架构加速推理。

Result: Nano变体在RadioML2018数据集上SNR>0 dB时达到86.5%平均准确率；在HT-CC数据集上达到94.2%准确率；参数量<10k；推理速度达92 μs/帧（FPGA/CPU协同），显著优于现有边缘模型。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer被证实为一种边缘高效的实时频谱智能解决方案，适用于物联网设备，兼顾了高精度、低参数量和快速推理，为边缘频谱监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [130] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种偏好条件式公交信号优先控制器，通过运行时调节偏好参数w来平衡公交优先与整体交通延误，无需重新训练，实验表明其性能优于固定时间控制、基于规则的TSP和固定权重PPO，且能保持约束可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习TSP方法优化固定奖励或固定标量化，当机构优先级随时段或中断条件变化时缺乏运行灵活性。

Method: 提出偏好条件式TSP控制器π(a|s,w)，在最小/最大绿灯时间和过渡可行性约束下选择下一信号相位，通过运行时偏好参数w调节公交优先权重；在IntersectionZoo上实现带约束的信号控制/TSP封装器，并扩展场景生成以处理训练中的稀疏公交优先事件。

Result: 单个学习到的条件策略跨运行时偏好生成平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于固定时间和基于规则的基线并保持约束可行性；尾延迟诊断显示非公交外部性在中等偏好设置下有限，但在高公交优先权重下会显著增加。

Conclusion: 偏好条件式TSP控制器实现了运行时灵活的公交优先权衡，无需重训练即可适应不同优先级需求，为实际部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [131] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BearingNAS，一种硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，用于在传感器裸片上进行轴承故障诊断。在极微预算（4-8 KiB RAM, 16-32 KiB Flash）下，通过无导数搜索策略和衰减核增长搜索空间，在笔记本电脑CPU上不到一小时完成搜索，在STMicroelectronics ISPU上达到99.50%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习推理能力直接嵌入传感器裸片（in-sensor processing），消除对昂贵GPU的依赖，实现低成本的工业级轴承故障诊断。

Method: 将NAS建模为约束优化问题，面向极微内存预算；采用轻量级无导数搜索策略替代GPU依赖；提出单数据流搜索空间，利用衰减核增长公式防止参数爆炸。

Result: 在笔记本电脑CPU上搜索时间<1小时；在STMicroelectronics LSM6DSO16IS ISPU上取得99.50%的诊断准确率。

Conclusion: 证明在传感器封装内完成ML工作负载的可行性，为低成本、生产级轴承故障诊断提供新路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [132] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分层边缘云系统中动态任务到达和异构资源导致的负载不均衡问题，提出一种两尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），联合优化服务部署、计算委派和功率控制，实现平均端到端时延降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%，收敛速度比传统PPO快约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层边缘云系统中动态任务到达与异构资源导致跨层负载不均衡，造成严重的排队时延和资源利用效率低下。

Method: 将联合服务部署、计算委派和功率控制（JSCP）问题分解为长期系统配置和短期资源分配两个子问题，提出两尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），利用变分自编码器压缩高维组合动作空间。

Result: 平均端到端时延降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%，收敛速度比传统PPO快约50%，性能接近分支定界最优解。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效适应动态网络条件，在时延和资源利用率方面显著优于无计算委派方案，且收敛效率高。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [133] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统化框架，用于从现有的表格异常检测数据集中设计可复现的持续异常检测（CAD）基准场景。该框架能够发现候选任务、过滤不合适的任务，并推导出能够揭示多样动态特性的有序任务序列。基于三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集，生成了五个基准场景，涵盖单数据集和多数据集CAD设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表格领域，持续异常检测（CAD）基准测试严重依赖于任务的定义、过滤、排序和验证方式，但任务边界很少被明确给出，任意的任务划分可能产生无法学习、冗余或过度可迁移的任务，从而掩盖真正的持续学习行为。

Method: 提出了一个系统化框架，包含三个步骤：发现候选任务、过滤不适合的任务、推导出能暴露多样动态特性的原则性任务排序。框架基于现有表格异常检测数据集设计可复现的基准场景。

Result: 从三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集（包含CIC-IDS-2017、CIC-IDS-2018、CSE-CIC-IDS-2018等）中，生成了五个基准场景，涵盖单数据集和多数据集两种CAD设置。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了CAD基准设计中任务划分随意性的问题，提供了可复现的基准场景设计方法，有助于更真实地评估持续异常检测模型的持续学习能力。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>


### [134] [SechKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Hyperbolic Secant Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18290)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SechKAN，一种基于双曲正割（sech）函数的KAN架构。该架构利用sech函数平滑的钟形曲线、局部响应和稳定梯度的特性，通过1D线性变换减少参数量，使模型规模与MLP相当。在函数拟合、偏微分方程问题以及MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100等图像分类任务中，SechKAN在参数量相近的情况下表现优于MLP和其他KAN变体，但运行时间略长于MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN网络在机器学习和科学计算中展现出有效性，但现有KAN架构仍有改进空间。作者旨在设计一种新的KAN架构，在保持模型参数量的同时提升性能。

Method: 采用双曲正割函数作为基函数，利用其平滑钟形、局部响应和稳定梯度的特性；通过1D线性变换减少参数量，使SechKAN与MLP的模型规模相当。

Result: 在函数拟合、PDE问题及MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100等图像分类任务中，SechKAN在参数量相近的情况下性能优于MLP和其他KAN变体；运行时间优于其他KAN变体，但略长于MLP。

Conclusion: SechKAN是一种有效的KAN架构，在多个任务上取得优于MLP和其他KAN变体的性能，但推理效率仍有提升空间。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [135] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双域融合长短期记忆（DDF-LSTM）模型，通过在网络架构中联合处理时间依赖和时间独立域信息，并设计改进的损失函数来增强对最小响应的敏感性，实现了高效准确的时间相关可靠性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理模型方法难以将时间独立随机变量与时间相关随机过程进行有效融合，无法捕捉两者之间的复杂交互作用，限制了时间相关可靠性分析的精度和效率。

Method: 提出DDF-LSTM模型：(1) 将时间独立变量嵌入到LSTM初始隐藏状态中；(2) 引入全连接层同时映射LSTM输出和时间独立变量到最终输出空间；(3) 设计改进的损失函数增强模型对最小响应的敏感性，提高失效概率估计精度。

Result: 四个案例验证了所提方法在计算效率和预测精度上的提升，训练后的DDF-LSTM模型能以最小计算代价通过蒙特卡洛仿真估计时间相关失效概率。

Conclusion: DDF-LSTM有效捕获了随机变量、随机过程与极限状态函数时间行为之间的依赖关系，实现了高效准确的时间相关可靠性分析。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains.
