</details>


### [170] [Adaptive Two-Stage Online Learning for Service-Affecting Failure Detection in Mobile Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18522)
*J. du Toit,G. Fita,J. Salzwedel,A. Stoltz,R. Wolhuter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段在线学习框架，用于移动核心网络的流量故障检测。第一阶段通过轻量级回归对正常流量动态进行增量建模，第二阶段分析预测残差并结合上下文指标检测真正的服务影响故障。该框架在全在线预序评估协议下运行，实现了最佳的精确率-召回率权衡，在可接受的假阳性率下获得了最高的召回率、F1分数和AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络运营商监控聚合流量以评估核心网络基础设施的运行健康状态，但由于强时间结构、非平稳性、测量伪影和极端类别不平衡，静态阈值监控方法效果有限，因此需要更可靠的故障检测方法。

Method: 两阶段在线学习框架：第一阶段使用带时间感知特征的轻量级回归增量建模正常流量动态；第二阶段分析预测残差并结合上下文指标检测真正的服务影响故障。采用预序评估协议实现全在线运行。

Result: 在线性和非线性模型上，提出的两阶段架构均实现了最佳的精确率-召回率权衡，在可接受的假阳性率下获得了最高的召回率、F1分数和AUC。

Conclusion: 明确的残差分解对于流式移动核心网络数据中的可靠故障检测至关重要。

Abstract: Mobile network operators monitor aggregated traffic volumes to assess the operational health of core network infrastructure. Reliable failure detection is challenging due to strong temporal structure, non-stationarity, measurement artefacts, and extreme class imbalance, which limit static threshold-based monitoring. This paper proposes a two-stage online learning framework for traffic-based failure detection in mobile core networks. Stage I incrementally models normal traffic dynamics using lightweight regression with time-aware features. Stage II analyses prediction residuals together with contextual indicators to detect genuine service-affecting network failures. The framework operates fully online under a prequential evaluation protocol, enabling continuous adaptation with low computational overhead. Across linear and non-linear models, the proposed two-stage architecture achieves the best precision-recall trade-off, attaining the highest recall, F1-score, and AUC at acceptable false positive rates. These results demonstrate the importance of explicit residual decomposition for reliable failure detection in streaming mobile core network data.

</details>


### [171] [Censoring-Aware In-Context Learning for Generalized Supplier Lead Time Estimation in Supply Chain Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18530)
*Christopher Wang,Sebastien Ouellet,Behrouz Haji Soleimani,Ali Etemad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LT-ICL模型，利用上下文学习与条件归一化流处理右删失供应商交货时间数据，在24个工业数据集上取得最优预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业交货时间数据存在天然右删失问题（部分订单在预测时尚未到达），传统回归/分类方法丢弃删失信息，生存模型需任务特定建模，缺乏高效通用的解决方案。

Method: LT-ICL结合Transformer主干网络与条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）头部，生成完整预测分布。在合成右删失任务上预训练，通过上下文学习实现对新工业数据集的零参数更新适应。

Result: 在7个行业24个专有供应链数据集上，LT-ICL在15/24个数据集上取得最低点预测误差，14/24个数据集上取得最低概率预测误差，平均排名最优。

Conclusion: 右删失概率预测是供应商交货时间预测的有效框架，预训练上下文模型可在低适应成本下为工业规划系统提供准确预测。

Abstract: Supplier lead time forecasting is a central input to material requirements planning, inventory optimization, and supply chain risk management. However, many industrial lead time datasets are naturally right-censored: at the time forecasts are required, some orders have not yet arrived. Standard regression and classification approaches discard this information, while conventional survival models require task-specific modeling. We propose LeadTime-ICL (LT-ICL), a censoring-aware in-context learning model for probabilistic lead time forecasting. LT-ICL combines a transformer backbone with a conditional normalizing-flow head, producing a full predictive distribution over lead times. The model is pretrained on synthetic right-censored lead time tasks, enabling in-context adaptation to new industrial datasets without task-specific parameter updates. We provide theoretical support for this formulation by showing that excess CRPS is bounded by prior misspecification and amortized approximation errors, providing clear direction for improving forecasting performance. We evaluate LT-ICL on 24 proprietary supply-chain datasets spanning seven industries. LT-ICL achieves the lowest point-forecasting error on 15 of the 24 datasets, and the lowest probabilistic forecasting error on 14 datasets, yielding the best average rank across both. These results support right-censored probabilistic forecasting as a practical formulation for supplier lead time prediction and demonstrate that pretrained in-context models can provide accurate, low-adaptation-cost forecasting for industrial planning systems.

</details>


### [172] [Operational Proto-Introspection in Looped Language Models: Process-Quality Taps, Executable Branching, and the Readout-Control Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18553)
*Jan Kirin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在冻结的循环变压器Ouro-RLTT上研究"操作原初内省"现象：通过探针可从隐藏状态预读模型计算质量（AUROC达0.797），但所有外部干预（分支/携带/修剪机制、方向引导、LoRA微调等）均无法转化为可验证的能力提升。模型可被读取但不可被操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型的隐藏状态是否包含"计算质量"的可读信号，以及外部干预能否将这些读出的信号转化为更好的推理结果。

Method: 在冻结的2.6B循环变压器Ouro-RLTT上，使用严格的前答案探针（排除答案区域和gold value）从隐藏状态预测GSM8K任务的成功概率；构建分支/携带/修剪机制（含分支缓存谱系、位精确残差捕获拼接）进行外部干预；对比方向引导、LoRA微调等干预手段；采用任务聚类95%置信区间、反对称评估等统计方法。

Result: 探针在隐藏状态加长度/log概率捷径上达AUROC 0.797（相比纯捷径0.731提升+0.066）；低容量探针实现分支存活0.9697、内容排名0.6310、正确性AUROC 0.7755；分支机制节省最多88%层计算量；所有冻结干预均无验证可行能力增益；定向引导为已确定负结果；LoRA改变表面行为但不提升净可达性。

Conclusion: 提出"操作原初内省"概念：模型隐藏状态包含可读的计算质量信号，但无法通过当前的外部干预手段将其转化为可验证的能力提升。模型并未"咨询"我们的探针，而是我们读取了其隐藏轨迹。

Abstract: Can a language model read the quality of ongoing computation, and can an external intervention turn that readout into better outcomes? We test both questions in a frozen 2.6B looped transformer, Ouro-RLTT. On GSM8K, a strict pre-answer probe excludes the answer region and gold value yet predicts eventual success: hidden states plus length and log-probability shortcuts reach AUROC 0.797, versus 0.731 for the shortcuts alone (incremental +0.066; task-clustered 95% CI [+0.021, +0.112]; 170 tasks, 680 candidates). Low-capacity taps also read role-specialized properties: task-disjoint branch survival reaches 0.9697 oracle retention, content ranking reaches 0.6310 macro top-1, and generated-branch correctness reaches AUROC 0.7755. A non-looped control replicates a candidate-quality readout, so recurrence is not required for every signal.
  We build branch/carry/prune machinery over Ouro's 192-slot recurrent cache, including branch-specific cache lineage and a bit-exact residual-capture splice that recomputes only the affected suffix and saves up to 88% of per-branch layer passes. No frozen intervention produces a validated capability gain. Directional steering is an established negative; a four-task matched-sampling comparison removes evidence for a frozen-fork gain but cannot estimate a general deficit; terminal selection remains unresolved and underpowered; and bounded LoRA changes surface behavior without improving net reachability. A two-null audit does not support the simplest span-misalignment explanation.
  We call this readable-but-not-yet-usable property operational proto-introspection. The model is not consulting our probes: we read its hidden trajectories, and our interventions fail to convert those readouts into validated capability. The pre-answer result is limited to one domain. Load-bearing values use source-item-disjoint splits and antisymmetrized evaluation where applicable.

</details>


### [173] [Robust Multi-View Classification under Noisy Supervision via Global Anchor Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18561)
*Yuliang Yang,Hongzhe Zhang,Huiru Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于全局锚点的标签审计方法（GALA），通过为每类每视图构建全局锚点、计算跨视图审计分数、自适应标签校正，有效抵抗多视图分类中的噪声标签问题，在六个数据集上优于八种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大部分多视图分类方法依赖准确标注，但在实际应用中噪声标签普遍存在，且现有方法从噪声监督中导出的修正信号会逐渐失去可靠性。

Method: 1) 为每个视图的每个类别构建全局锚点，聚合整类样本以提供对个体预测不敏感的稳定参考；2) 每个视图衡量实例到其观测标签锚点与最近竞争锚点的接近程度，并将各视图评估与分类器置信度融合得到跨视图审计分数；3) 根据审计分数对可疑样本赋予小权重，并在锚点候选与分类器预测一致时执行自适应标签校正；4) 校正后的标签反过来优化锚点并监督抗噪声的表示学习。

Result: 在六个数据集上的大量实验表明，GALA在八种现有方法中表现最优，尤其在高噪声率场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: GALA通过全局锚点机制和跨视图审计策略有效抵抗了噪声标签对多视图分类的负面影响，实现了鲁棒的标签校正和表示学习。

Abstract: In recent years, multi-view learning has attracted increasing attention, as it integrates the complementary information of heterogeneous views. Most existing multi-view classification methods rely on accurate annotations to guarantee performance. However, noisy labels are ubiquitous in practice due to imperfect annotation, and the refinement signals that existing methods derive from models trained on such noisy supervision can gradually lose their reliability. To deal with this problem, we propose a novel Global Anchor-based Label Auditing method (GALA) for multi-view classification to resist the negative impact of noisy labels. Specifically, we construct a global anchor for each class in every view, which aggregates the samples of the whole class and thus offers a stable reference insensitive to individual predictions. Then, each view measures how close an instance is to the anchor of its observed label relative to the nearest competing anchor, and the per-view evaluations are fused with the classifier confidence into a cross-view audit score. Based on the audit scores, suspicious samples are assigned small weights, and an adaptive correction strategy rewrites a label only when the anchor-based candidate agrees with the classifier prediction. Finally, the corrected labels in turn refine the anchors and supervise noise-robust representation learning. Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that GALA outperforms eight state-of-the-art methods, especially under high noise rates.

</details>


### [174] [AMICA-Python: Adaptive Mixture Independent Component Analysis with Anderson Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18568)
*Scott Huberty,Christian O'Reilly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMICA-Python，一个基于Python的AMICA算法实现，具备scikit-learn兼容API，支持Anderson加速，在14个公开EEG数据集上验证了与原始Fortran实现高度一致的数值精度（中位相对绝对差1.07×10⁻⁸）和更快的运行速度（标准版快17.7%，加速版快34.1%），使AMICA能够方便地集成到Python科学计算生态中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMICA算法在EEG盲源分离中性能优异，但其实现长期依赖单一的Fortran代码并通过EEGLAB/MATLAB访问，限制了非MATLAB生态下分析流程的可及性与可扩展性。

Method: 采用Python重写AMICA算法，遵循参考Fortran实现，提供scikit-learn风格的API；引入可选的Anderson加速方案加速收敛；在14个公开EEG记录上进行3次重复实验，对比数值精度和运行时间。

Result: AMICA-Python与Fortran参考实现的最终归一化对数似然中位数均为11.572，归一化相对绝对差中位数仅1.07×10⁻⁸；标准版运行速度快17.7%，Anderson加速版快34.1%。

Conclusion: AMICA-Python以高数值精度复现了参考实现，运行时间具有竞争力，并通过更易访问和扩展的Python接口使AMICA算法更广泛可用。

Abstract: Adaptive Mixture Independent Component Analysis (AMICA) is widely used in EEG research and has long been associated with strong empirical performance for blind source separation. Despite its impact, practical use has historically depended on a single Fortran implementation, accessed via the EEGLAB toolbox for MATLAB, limiting its accessibility for analytical pipelines not designed within the MATLAB ecosystem. Here we present AMICA-Python, a Python implementation of the AMICA algorithm, with a scikit-learn-conformant API designed for integration with existing scientific Python pipelines. The implementation follows the reference algorithm closely while adopting modern software engineering practices and an interface familiar to Python users. Additionally, we introduce an optional Anderson acceleration scheme that can dramatically reduce the time to convergence for this relatively slow algorithm. To evaluate numerical agreement and practical performance, we benchmarked AMICA-Python against the reference Fortran implementation on 14 open EEG recordings. After averaging 3 runs of each implementation on all 14 recordings, AMICA-Python closely matched the reference, with a median final normalized log-likelihoods of 11.572 for both the Fortran and Python implementations, and a negligible median relative absolute difference of only $1.07\times10^{-8}$ when normalized by the absolute Fortran value. Runtime was also competitive. Relative to the reference implementation, AMICA-Python was 17.7\% faster, while the Anderson-accelerated variant was 34.1\% faster. AMICA-Python reproduces the reference implementation to high numerical precision with competitive runtime, while making AMICA available through a more accessible and extensible Python interface.

</details>


### [175] [Conditioned Direct Feedback Alignment via Activity and Error Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18574)
*Houman Safaai,Varun Reddy,Bernardo L. Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了直接反馈对齐（DFA）训练中局部外积权重更新的两种失败模式：活动条件（presynaptic-activity factor）和误差条件（local-error factor），并提出归一化DFA（nDFA）家族（活动nDFA、误差nDFA、K-nDFA）通过左右预处理（逆二阶矩）来缓解各向异性问题。实验表明活动条件化增益约40个百分点，误差条件化提升1.77-7.53个百分点，两者结合额外增加0.40-0.90个百分点，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上得到验证。文章强调这是因子级研究，而非BP的通用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明DFA在反馈质量上逊于BP，但本文关注一个被忽视的失败模式：即使反馈方向正确，由于局部权重更新是外积计算，各向异性可通过突触前活动因子或局部误差因子引入，导致学习失效。作者旨在分离并量化这两种失败模式的独立与联合效应。

Method: 通过受控合成实验分离活动条件化失败模式（高方差方向包含任务无关噪声），在tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST、ReLU/softmax MNIST和预注册Fashion-MNIST上验证。提出nDFA家族：活动nDFA用逆活动二阶矩右预处理，误差nDFA用逆局部误差二阶矩左预处理，K-nDFA结合两者并独立调阻尼。理论分析包括线性化对齐后精确输入侧谱恒等式和Kronecker因子动机。

Result: 活动条件化增益约40个百分点；误差条件化提升raw DFA 1.77-7.53个百分点；两者结合在活动条件化基础上额外增加0.40-0.90个百分点。8个新种子下ReLU/softmax MNIST结果可复制。误差因子欠阻尼时脆弱，BatchNorm是强活动侧替代方案，卷积网络增益仍不完全。

Conclusion: DFA失败不能仅归因于反馈质量，局部外积更新的各向异性是独立且重要的失败源。条件化DFA提供了因子级分析框架，但并非BP的通用替代品，也不解决全层卷积的信用分配问题。

Abstract: Direct feedback alignment (DFA) trains hidden layers with fixed random projections of the output error, avoiding the transposed-weight backward pass of backpropagation (BP). We study a failure mode of DFA training that is distinct from feedback quality: the local weight update is calculated by an outer product, so anisotropy can enter through either its presynaptic-activity factor or its local-error factor. Our analyses with controlled synthetic regimes isolate the first failure mode and show an approximately 40-percentage-point activity-conditioning gain when high-variance directions contain task-irrelevant nuisance. Three clean confirmations isolate a different regime: error conditioning improves raw DFA by 1.77--7.53 percentage points, and combining independently selected activity and error factors adds 0.40--0.90 points over activity conditioning. The signs hold for tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST and preregistered Fashion-MNIST, and replicate on eight fresh seeds in a ReLU/softmax MNIST model. This factorization yields a symmetric block-local family of normalized DFA (nDFA): activity nDFA right-preconditions by an inverse activity second moment, error nDFA left-preconditions by an inverse local-error second moment, and K-nDFA applies both factors with separately tuned damping. A linearized post-alignment calculation gives an exact input-side spectral identity and a Kronecker-factor motivation for the two-sided rule, whereas norm matching rules out a scalar step-size explanation. The error factor is fragile when under-damped, BatchNorm is a strong activity-side alternative, and convnet gains remain partial. We therefore frame conditioned DFA as a factor-level study of when local outer-product rules fail, not as a general replacement for BP or a solution to all-layer convolutional credit assignment.

</details>


### [176] [On the Diverse Dynamical Behaviors Arising in Deep Linear Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18584)
*Sixu Li,Thomas Jacob Maranzatto,Jan Peszek,Trevor Teolis,Semih Akkoc,Konstantin Riedl,Sennur Ulukus,Nicolás García Trillos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过交互粒子系统视角研究深度线性编码器型Transformer的推理时行为。在嵌入维度为2时，证明其动力学可重构为广义Kuramoto模型，并利用Watanabe-Strogatz理论揭示其固有低维性。在Ott-Antonsen流形上，参数矩阵可诱导聚类、振荡和分岔等长期行为，并通过揭示隐藏哈密顿结构刻画了振荡与分岔。结构稳定性结果表明流形附近的动力学与流形上的动力学具有相同长期行为。高维数值实验表明这些行为在高维中依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度线性Transformer在推理时的动力学行为，揭示其内在机制和长期演化规律，建立与物理系统中粒子相互作用模型的联系。

Method: 将Transformer tokens建模为交互粒子系统，利用Watanabe-Strogatz理论分析低维动力学，引入Ott-Antonsen流形进行解析分析，发现哈密顿结构并通过结构稳定性理论推广到一般初始化，最后进行高维数值验证。

Result: 在嵌入维度2时，线性Transformer动力学可简化为广义Kuramoto模型；在OA流形上发现了聚类、振荡和分岔等多种长期行为；证明了哈密顿结构的存在；展示了结构稳定性；高维数值实验初步验证了理论结果的普适性。

Conclusion: 线性编码器型Transformer的推理动力学具有丰富的低维结构，可通过粒子系统理论和动力系统方法进行深入分析，为理解Transformer行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study the inference-time behavior of deep linear encoder-only transformers through the lens of interacting particle systems. In this perspective, tokens are modeled as particles that interact dynamically through successive linear self-attention layers. We show that in embedding dimension two, for any key, query, and value matrices, the dynamics can be reformulated as a generalized Kuramoto-type model with pure second-harmonic coupling. This formulation is amenable to Watanabe--Strogatz theory which reveals the dynamics are intrinsically low-dimensional regardless of the parameter matrices. For a class of token initializations associated with the Ott--Antonsen (OA) manifold, we show that the parameter matrices induce a diverse variety of long-time behaviors in linear transformers, including clustering, oscillations, and bifurcations. The oscillations and bifurcations are characterized by uncovering a hidden Hamiltonian structure in the dynamics. By establishing a structural stability result, we further show that dynamics initialized near the OA manifold exhibit the same long-time behavior as those initialized exactly on the manifold. Motivated by our theory in dimension two, we conduct numerical experiments for analogous parameter regimes in higher-dimensional transformers. Our numerical experiments suggest that the long-time behaviors characterized in our theoretical results persist in higher dimensions.

</details>


### [177] [Planning as Emergent Behavior in Reinforcement Learning with Relational Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18589)
*Armin Sommer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在无模型强化学习中，规划能力的涌现取决于神经架构的隐状态结构。只有当网络具有关系型隐状态（每个状态锚定一个环境状态并通过学习到的关系交换消息）时，才能恢复环境转移结构并实现规划。而对照组中需要自行发现状态对应关系的智能体则无法产生规划。这解释了无模型RL中规划涌现的现象，并推测人脑中可能也存在类似的神经架构先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现无模型强化学习中也能涌现规划行为，但其背后的条件尚不明确。本文旨在揭示这种涌现的底层机制，特别是神经架构隐状态结构所起的关键作用。

Method: 通过对比实验，设计两种智能体：一种是具有关系型隐状态的网络，每个隐状态锚定一个环境状态并通过学习到的关系交换消息；另一种是对照组，需要自行发现哪些细胞对应哪些状态。观察两者在规划行为上的差异。

Result: 关系型隐状态网络能够恢复环境的转移结构，在决策时通过学习到的关系图进行规划，从而提升策略性能。而对照组无法形成这种绑定关系，也无法产生规划行为。

Conclusion: 无模型强化学习中规划行为的涌现取决于关系型隐状态的神经架构先验，该机制可能解释了人脑中从纯奖励最大化中涌现规划的现象。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is conventionally divided into model-based and model-free methods. In this taxonomy, model-based methods perform lookahead planning over a learned world model, whereas model-free methods learn a reactive state-action mapping. Recent work, however, has shown that planning can emerge from model-free reinforcement learning alone. The conditions under which this behavior emerges from a pure reward-maximization objective have so far remained unclear. In this paper, we present evidence that, in the observed cases, the hidden-state structure of the neural architecture is the deciding factor. We find that a network of relational hidden states, each anchored to an environment state and exchanging messages along learned relations, acquires a planning mechanism. These hidden states recover the environment's transition structure in their learned relations, and improve the policy at decision time by planning over the learned graph. In a matched control agent that must additionally discover which cells represent which states, no such binding arises, and no planning follows from it. We argue that this explains the observed phenomenon of emergent planning in model-free reinforcement learning and raises the question of how common such emergent planning might be more generally. Finally, we hypothesize that the discovered mechanism could describe how planning emerges from pure reward maximization in the human brain through a neural architectural prior.

</details>


### [178] [Mark, Don't Erase: Token Inoculation for Dual-Use Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18639)
*Seunghyun Lee,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Token Inoculation 通过在持续预训练中为危险内容插入特殊标记（绑定），并在监督微调中训练模型根据标记存在与否决定回答或拒绝（分支），实现了在保留危险知识的同时进行选择性拒绝，在安全-效用权衡上优于遗忘和拒绝训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全干预方法（遗忘、过滤、拒绝训练）在移除或压制危险知识时，会损害相邻领域能力或导致过度拒绝。作者认为正确的操作应是条件化（conditioning）而非缩减（reduction），即在保留危险知识的前提下实现行为级门控。

Method: Token Inoculation 包含两个阶段：1）绑定阶段（持续预训练）：在双用途文档中插入特殊标记，使模型将标记与危险领域的底层语义绑定；2）分支阶段（监督微调）：训练模型在标记存在时正确回答危险查询，标记缺失时拒绝回答，从而实现选择性拒绝。

Result: 在危险领域（如 WMDP-Bio）上，Token Inoculation 将准确率从 79% 降至 18%，同时保留了基础模型 93% 的良性领域性能（如 MMLU），在 1B-14B 模型规模上均取得最佳安全-效用权衡。拒绝选择性可通过条件信号质量控制，且预训练阶段的语义绑定对泛化至关重要。

Conclusion: 安全对齐应被视作条件化问题而非遗忘问题：在受控访问下保留敏感知识比将其摧毁能实现更精确的行为控制。

Abstract: Safety interventions on dual-use knowledge typically choose between destroying hazardous content (e.g., unlearning, filtering) and suppressing it at the output layer (e.g., refusal training); both pay a tax in adjacent-domain competence or over-refusal. We argue that the right operation is conditioning, not reduction: we show that hazardous knowledge can be retained in the model and behaviorally gated by a privileged control token. Our method, Token Inoculation, introduces a binding-and-branching approach. First, during continued pre-training, we mark hazardous content by inserting a special token alongside dual-use documents, so the model binds the marker to the underlying semantics of the hazardous domain. Second, during supervised fine-tuning, we teach the model to answer hazardous queries correctly when the special token is present and to refuse them when it is absent, thereby enabling selective refusal without removing dual-use knowledge. On hazardous domain (e.g., WMDP-Bio), Token Inoculation reduces accuracy from 79% to 18% while retaining 93% of the base-model's benign-domain performance (e.g., MMLU), achieving the best safety-utility trade-off against unlearning and refusal-tuning baselines across 1B-14B model scales. We further show that refusal selectivity is controllable through the quality of the conditioning signal and that domain-specific semantic binding during pre-training is critical for the conditional behavior to generalize beyond memorized triggers. Our results suggest that safety alignment is better cast as a conditioning problem than a forgetting one: behavioral control is more precise when sensitive knowledge is retained under controlled access than when it is destroyed.

</details>


### [179] [A Self-Evolving Default Action for Cooperative Tasks with Continuous Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18597)
*Shuangyao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAFE提出了一种基于自我进化默认动作的反事实基线方法，用于连续动作空间的多智能体强化学习，解决了蒙特卡洛采样带来的偏差问题，并在合作式车辆任务中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实信用分配方法在连续动作空间中存在局限性：通过蒙特卡洛采样近似反事实基线会引入策略梯度偏差，且无法保证收敛到局部最优，因为采样动作可能未被充分训练。

Method: 提出SAFE框架，使用基于每个智能体经验缓冲区中自我进化默认动作的反事实基线，无需额外仿真、奖励模型或环境先验知识，天然适用于连续动作空间。

Result: 在合作式车辆任务上的大量实验表明，SAFE持续优于最先进的模型。

Conclusion: 该基线能准确量化每个智能体的贡献，且不会向确定性策略梯度引入偏差，确保收敛到局部最优。

Abstract: Counterfactual credit assignment has proven effective in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) for discrete action spaces, yet its extension to continuous-action cooperative tasks remains challenging. Existing methods that approximate the counterfactual baseline via Monte Carlo sampling often introduce bias into policy gradients and fail to guarantee convergence to local optima, as the sampled actions may not have been sufficiently trained. To address these limitations, we propose SAFE, a novel MARL framework that employs a counterfactual baseline conditioned on a self-evolving default action sampled from each agent's experience buffer. This design naturally extends to continuous action spaces without relying on additional simulations, reward models, or environment-specific prior knowledge. The baseline accurately quantifies each agent's contribution, and introduces no bias into the deterministic policy gradient, ensuring convergence to local optima. Extensive experiments on cooperative vehicular tasks demonstrate that SAFE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [180] [BRIDGE: Bottleneck-Aware Regulator-Set Inference and Diagnosis for Cooperative Gene Regulatory Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18602)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Clayton Thomas Barham,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIDGE和TRACE是用于协同基因调控网络中完整调控子集恢复与诊断的框架。Residual HOS2方法通过残差高阶集评分改进了协同调控子集的恢复效果，但仍存在精确恢复率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因调控网络推断方法大多输出成对调控因子-靶标排名，无法捕捉多个调控因子协同作用的完整调控子集，且缺乏对失败原因的归因分析工具。

Method: 提出BRIDGE框架用于完整调控子集恢复，以及TRACE诊断套件用于归因失败瓶颈。TRACE包含无泄漏机制不匹配协同性压力测试，Residual HOS2在原始表达向量上操作而不依赖手工设计的乘积相关性特征。

Result: 在30个匹配的种子-协同性设置下，Residual HOS2相较于PairS2将Jaccard相似度从0.382提升至0.460，召回率从0.522提升至0.597，精确恢复率从0.053提升至0.113。PairS2提案后接Residual HOS2重排序可将HOS2评分的候选集减少94-97%，同时基本保持精确恢复行为。

Conclusion: 边缘排序、候选检索、集水平评分和精确协同调控子集恢复是四个不同的目标。候选覆盖是必要但不充分的，集水平误排名仍然是精确恢复失败的主要来源。

Abstract: Cooperative gene regulation often depends on groups of regulators acting jointly, but most gene regulatory network (GRN) inference methods output pairwise regulator-target rankings. We introduce Bottleneck-Aware Regulator-Set Inference and Diagnosis (BRIDGE), a framework for complete regulator-set recovery, and Targeted Recovery Attribution for Cooperative Evaluation (TRACE), a diagnostic suite that attributes failures to retrieval, set-level scoring, decoding, and evaluation bottlenecks.
  TRACE includes a leak-free mechanism-mismatch cooperativity stress test in which cooperative targets are generated by random nonlinear mechanisms rather than product interactions. This design avoids feature-mechanism circularity: Residual higher-order set scoring (Residual HOS2) operates on raw expression vectors without handcrafted product-correlation features. Across 30 matched seed-cooperativity settings, Residual HOS2 improves Jaccard similarity from 0.382 to 0.460, recall from 0.522 to 0.597, and exact recovery from 0.053 to 0.113 over a decomposable pairwise set scorer (PairS2), although exact recovery remains low.
  On SERGIO DS3, oracle retrieval and TRACE show that candidate coverage is necessary but insufficient because set-level misranking remains the dominant source of exact-recovery failure. PairS2 proposal followed by Residual HOS2 reranking reduces HOS2-scored candidate sets by 94-97% while largely preserving exact-recovery behavior. These results distinguish edge ranking, candidate retrieval, set-level scoring, and exact cooperative regulator-set recovery as separate objectives.

</details>


### [181] [Stale but Stable: Staleness-Adaptive Trust Regions for Stabilizing Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18722)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Xiangxin Zhou,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Staleness-Adaptive Trust Region (SAT)，一种通过自适应裁剪区间来缓解异步强化学习中策略陈旧性（staleness）导致的训练-推理不匹配问题的方法。SAT 利用采样的对数比率作为陈旧性代理，对高不匹配尾部进行核缩放收缩，在 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base 的异步 RL 设置中，SAT-GSPO w/ R3 在 AIME24 avg@8 上达到 lag 1 时 35.83、lag 8 时 34.79 的最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习中，推理与训练解耦带来的陈旧性（staleness）不可避免，且 PPO 的 clipping 机制仅作为采样代理而非全策略约束，在高陈旧性场景下对策略更新的控制力不足，导致训练-推理散度加剧，近似误差增大。

Method: 提出 Staleness-Adaptive Trust Region (SAT)：使用分离的采样对数比率作为陈旧性代理；通过基于陈旧性的核缩放识别批次内的高不匹配尾部；仅收缩名义 PPO 区间中符号选择的一端，在普通 token 上保持基线行为，在新拦截的向外带上执行更保守的更新。证明了局部区间包含性和相对于 PPO 的逐点悲观性。

Result: 在基于 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base 的异步 RL 设置中（SGLang 推理引擎 + Megatron 训练），SAT-GSPO w/ R3 达到 AIME24 avg@8 最佳结果：lag 1 时 35.83，lag 8 时 34.79；SAT-GSPO 在 lag 1 时达到 34.17。

Conclusion: 将裁剪区间与陈旧性异质性对齐能有效稳定异步 RL；自适应裁剪和路由重放作为互补的稳定器，分别针对不匹配尾部和路由不一致性。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning improves throughput by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but staleness is an inevitable byproduct compounded by policy lag, engine delays, and mixture-of-experts routing. From a trust-region perspective, this mismatch is critical: training-inference divergence governs approximation error in finite-horizon bounds, whereas PPO clipping only gates sampled outward updates, acting as a sampled surrogate rather than a full-policy constraint. As a result, high-staleness updates remain weakly controlled in the asynchronous regime where stale rollouts matter most.
  We introduce the Staleness-Adaptive Trust Region (SAT), which uses the detached sampled log-ratio as a practical staleness proxy, identifies high-mismatch tails within each batch via staleness-based kernel scaling, and contracts only the sign-selected endpoint of the nominal PPO interval. This preserves baseline behavior on ordinary tokens while enforcing more conservative updates on newly intercepted outward bands. We prove local interval containment and pointwise pessimism relative to PPO, showing how the adaptive rule reshapes update geometry under heterogeneous staleness.
  We evaluate SAT in a decoupled asynchronous RL setup built on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, using SGLang as the inference engine and Megatron for training. In this setting, SAT-GSPO w/ R3 achieves the best observed AIME24 avg@8, reaching 35.83 at lag 1 and 34.79 at lag 8, while SAT-GSPO reaches 34.17 at lag 1. Adaptive clipping and routing replay act as complementary stabilizers targeting mismatch tails and routing inconsistency, respectively. Overall, aligning clip intervals with staleness heterogeneity effectively stabilizes asynchronous RL.

</details>


### [182] [Graph Neural Network-based Algorithm Selection for the Traveling Salesman Problem: A Systematic Study of Cost and Rank Losses under Distinct Budget Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18632)
*Zhaoxuan Li,Jiale Yang,Yifei Lu,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GNNAS-TSP框架，利用图神经网络直接从图数据学习TSP实例表示，将算法选择建模为联合成本预测与排序任务，在10秒和60秒预算下均优于单一最优求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行商问题中求解器性能高度依赖实例特征，传统算法选择依赖手工特征工程，缺乏自动化和泛化能力。

Method: 基于GNN的算法选择框架，将AS建模为成本预测与排序联合任务，评估了基于成本（MSE、MAE、Huber）、基于排序（RankNet、ListNet、LambdaRank）及混合学习目标。

Result: 在10秒和60秒预算下，所选配置在归一化解成本上均优于单最优求解器（SBS）；10秒预算下成本改进显著且具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: GNNAS-TSP是一种有效的元求解策略，适用于求解器性能存在可开发差异的场景。

Abstract: Automated Algorithm Selection (AS) aims to improve problem-solving performance by selecting, for each problem instance, the most suitable algorithm from a predefined portfolio. This is particularly relevant to the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP), where solver performance is strongly instance-dependent. We introduce GNNAS-TSP, a Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based AS framework that learns TSP instance representations directly from raw graph data, avoiding manual feature engineering. GNNAS-TSP formulates AS as a joint cost-prediction and ranking task. We evaluate cost-based (mean squared error (MSE), mean absolute error (MAE), and Huber), rank-based (RankNet, ListNet, and LambdaRank), and hybrid learning objectives for a portfolio comprising Chained Lin-Kernighan, Edge Assembly Crossover, Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun, Multiagent Optimization System, and Concorde. Experiments use fixed computational budgets of 10 and 60 seconds. On the held-out test set, the selected configurations improve on the Single Best Solver (SBS) in normalized solution cost at both budgets. For the 10s budget, AS achieves substantial and statistically significant cost improvement over SBS. Overall, the results suggest that GNNAS-TSP is a useful meta-solving strategy when exploitable variation exists across solver performance.

</details>


### [183] [Is EEG-to-Text Feasible in Real-World Scenarios? An In-Depth Analysis Using a Neuropsychology-Inspired Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18749)
*Zihan Zhang,Yu Bao,Xiao Ding,Tianyi Jiang,Kai Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有EEG2Text模型依赖教师强制评估（teacher-forcing），无法在真实场景中应用。本文发现原因是现有基准忽略了EEG的不稳定性，通过神经心理学范式实验证明无教师强制解码的可行性，并构建了128通道高密度EEG数据集COFETT，实现了无需教师强制的稳健解码评估，为实际EEG2Text应用开辟道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决EEG2Text领域长期存在的争议——EEG是否包含可解码的语言信息。现有模型严重依赖teacher-forcing评估而非teacher-forcing时无法生成有意义解码，这阻碍了EEG2Text在真实非学术场景中的应用。

Method: 采用神经心理学启发的实验范式，发现现有基准忽略了EEG的不稳定性（EEG instability）这一混淆因素；使用128通道高密度EEG帽构建COFETT数据集，支持无teacher-forcing的稳健评估。

Result: 实验证明了无teacher-forcing的EEG2Text解码的可行性；COFETT在区分模型性能方面达到SOTA水平，并能实现稳健的无teacher-forcing评估。

Conclusion: EEG确实包含可解码的语言信息，现有争议源于基准设计缺陷；COFETT为实际EEG2Text应用提供了可靠评估基准，有望推动该领域从学术研究走向真实应用。

Abstract: Translating brain signals into text could restore communication for people with severe paralysis, yet practically usable systems to date rely on invasive electrocorticography (ECoG). Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a non-invasive alternative, and EEG-to-text (EEG2Text) has been widely explored. Interestingly, however, EEG2Text models generally rely on teacher-forcing evaluation; without it, they fail to generate meaningful decoding. This reliance prevents EEG2Text from being applied in real-world, non-academic settings. This has fueled numerous debates about whether EEG2Text is a meaningful direction, by extension, and whether EEG truly contains decodable linguistic information. Here, using a neuropsychology-informed paradigm, we find that existing EEG2Text benchmarks have neglected EEG instability, a flaw that has confounded inference and sparked debate. Our experiments furnish key evidence for the feasibility of teacher-forcing-free EEG2Text decoding. Accordingly, we assemble the Corpus OF Eeg-To-Text (COFETT) using a 128-channel high-density EEG cap, providing a benchmark dedicated to evaluating EEG2Text models. In comparisons with multiple existing benchmarks, COFETT achieves SOTA ability to distinguish among model performances and enables robust, teacher-forcing-free evaluation, thereby opening a path toward practical EEG2Text applications. COFETT is open sourced in https://github.com/baoyudu/COFETT.

</details>


### [184] [Spaghetti Architect: A Contamination-Resistant, By-Construction-Labelled, Multi-Language Code Dataset Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18642)
*Yuxiang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spaghetti Architect，一种通过反优化转译将干净中间表示映射为冗余扁平化程序、并按两个正交难度轴标注的代码数据集生成工具，解决了现有代码语料库缺乏可控性、参考标准和抗污染能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有挖掘式代码语料库缺乏控制性：语义杂乱、难度不可控、缺少已知最优参考标准，且公开样本可能已存在于模型训练集中导致污染。

Method: 设计反优化转译器，将语言无关的 JSON 中间表示映射为五个语言（Python、JavaScript、Go、Java、C++）的冗余全扁平化程序；通过严格嵌套的反模式配置控制代码杂乱程度；按内在难度（问题规模）和附带难度（固定语义下的呈现形式）两个正交轴标注；使用私有保留种子生成新变体以抵抗污染。

Result: 质量顺序与现有复杂度和可读性指标一致；四模型开放阶梯测试中，精确匹配随模型规模提升，内在难度旋钮将最强模型的算术聚合准确率降至零；开发集分数与重新生成的保留集分数差异≤0.012（理解）和≤0.011（重构）；重构等价性（0.73→0.99）呈规模不变性而输出预测崩溃；消融自标注使最弱模型下降0.173而最强仅下降0.017。

Conclusion: Spaghetti Architect 提供可控、正确可保证、带难度标注且抗污染的代码数据集生成方案，开源发布（MIT协议）。

Abstract: Mined code corpora are abundant but uncontrolled: a snippet's semantics, surface "messiness," and difficulty are whatever the wild contained; there is no known-optimal reference to grade against; and any public sample may already sit in a model's training set. We present Spaghetti Architect, a tool that mints code datasets with the control such corpora lack. An anti-optimization transpiler maps a clean, language-agnostic JSON intermediate representation to deliberately redundant, fully-flattened programs in five languages (Python, JavaScript, Go, Java, C++); every program is compiled, run, and checked against a reference oracle, so each instance is correct by construction. The clean IR is a known-optimal reference, messiness is dialed by strictly-nested anti-pattern profiles, each instance is labelled along two orthogonal difficulty axes, intrinsic (problem size) and incidental (presentation at fixed semantics), and contamination is resisted by minting fresh variants from a private held-out seed. We give construct-validity evidence that the quality order moves established complexity and readability metrics, and report baselines on a four-model open ladder: exact match rises with scale, and the intrinsic knob collapses arithmetic-aggregation accuracy of even the strongest model to zero. Further, development-set scores equal freshly re-minted held-out counterparts within $|Δ|\le 0.012$ (comprehension) and $\le 0.011$ (refactoring); on identical programs, refactoring equivalence ($0.73 \rightarrow 0.99$) is scale-invariant while output prediction collapses; and ablating the generator's self-annotations shows they inflate the weakest model an order of magnitude more than the strongest ($-0.173$ vs $-0.017$): the annotated ladder resolves one of three adjacent pairs where the unannotated resolves all three. Open source (MIT), dependency-free, archived under a persistent DOI.

</details>


### [185] [Exposure-Based Reinforcement Learning to Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18689)
*Harrie Oosterhuis,Rolf Jagerman,Zhen Qin,Xuanhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于暴露度（exposure-based）的强化学习排序方法，通过基线校正、部分边际化和GPU计算实现高样本效率，并利用文档暴露分布实现与自动微分的无缝集成，在更快的收敛速度和更高的排序性能上超越了现有自定义梯度方法，且无稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法应用于学习排序时面临巨大的动作空间，导致计算效率低下；虽然自定义梯度计算算法能提升效率，但实现复杂且与自动微分不兼容，限制了实际应用。

Method: 放弃自定义梯度，转而聚焦方差缩减和GPU计算；通过基线校正和部分边际化实现高样本效率；提出将梯度估计置于文档暴露分布背后的抽象层，使得排序损失可作为暴露的可微函数，从而利用自动微分进行优化。

Result: 新方法在收敛速度和最终排序性能上显著优于现有自定义梯度方法，且GPU计算时间无额外成本；现有自定义梯度方法在多轮训练中存在严重稳定性问题，而新方法无此问题。

Conclusion: 该工作通过提升有效性、效率和易用性，显著改进了强化学习用于学习排序的方法论。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) methods for learning-to-rank (LTR) can optimize (almost) any ranking goal, e.g., from precision or discounted cumulative gain to fairness-of-exposure or ranking distillation. However, standard RL is ineffective and computationally costly due to the enormous action space in LTR settings. Existing methods reach computational efficiency through custom gradient computation algorithms, but they are very complex to implement and often clash with auto-differentiation. Consequently, existing RL for LTR is not attractive to many practitioners. We reconsider RL for LTR while actively avoiding reliance on custom gradients. Contrary to the existing approaches, we focus on variance reduction and GPU computation. In doing so, we discover that high sample-efficiency can be reached through baseline corrections and partial marginalization. Furthermore, we propose an abstraction that places gradient estimation behind a document-exposure distribution, this enables seamless plug-and-play integration with auto-differentiation. Thereby, one only has to implement a loss as a differentiable function of exposure and RL for LTR can optimize it using auto-differentiation. Our experimental results reveal that our new exposure-based RL for LTR approach converges considerably faster and at significantly higher ranking performance than existing custom gradients, with no additional costs in computation time when using GPUs. In contrast, existing custom gradients result in severe stability issues when converging over many epochs, which never occur for our methods. Thus, we considerably improve RL for LTR methodology by increasing its effectiveness, efficiency, and ease of application.

</details>


### [186] [HindsightBench: A Black-Box Behavioral Audit Protocol for Parametric Hindsight in Time-Indexed LLM Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18867)
*Haozhe Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HindsightBench，一种黑盒行为审计协议，能在无回测、无logprobs、无语料访问的条件下，以探针级成本检测大语言模型在时序金融决策任务中的参数化后见之明。对7家厂商15个模型在258节点宏观面板上审计发现：日期触发反射与训练代际相关而非规模；有效知识截止日期比厂商报告早最多8个月；审计结果对服务量化精度和推理模式敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会泄露已实现结果的参数化知识到历史金融决策任务中，但缺乏廉价的黑盒审计方法检测这种后见之明偏差。

Method: 构建四臂日期操控矩阵（揭示/仅日期/掩码/移植）、双重记忆探针（日期恢复和结果回忆）、六项逐模型指标（触发强度、移植效应、截止后安慰剂、可恢复性、行为有效知识截止、回忆-准确率解离系数），并设置数据依赖可识别性门控条件。

Result: 三个主要发现：(i) 日期触发反射与训练代际相关而非规模——2024代模型从1B到70B均不存在，而所有测试的2026代模型均存在；(ii) 有效知识截止日期跨厂商跨度达22个月，比厂商报告日期早最多8个月；(iii) 审计结果对服务配置敏感，BF16服务会破坏触发估计稳定性而AWQ-INT4保留之。

Conclusion: HindsightBench能够有效检测LLM的后见之明偏差，但审计协议需附带操作要求（固定量化精度和推理模式，公开解析器和采样策略）。

Abstract: Large language models leak parametric knowledge of realized outcomes into historical financial decision tasks. Existence is settled; what users lack is a cheap way to audit a given model for it. We present HindsightBench, a black-box behavioral audit protocol that profiles parametric hindsight in any time-indexed LLM decision task at probe-level cost (no backtests, no logprobs, no corpus access). The protocol chains a four-arm date-manipulation matrix (revealed/date-only/masked/transplanted), dual memory probes (date recovery; outcome recall), and six per-model metrics -- trigger strength, transplant effect, post-cutoff placebo, recoverability, behaviorally effective knowledge cutoff, and a recall-accuracy dissociation coefficient -- with explicit gates where identifiability is data-dependent. Applying it to 15 models from seven vendors on a 258-node vintage-correct macro panel yields three headline patterns: (i) the date-trigger reflex tracks training generation, not scale -- absent across the 2024 open-weight generation from 1B to 70B, present in every tested 2026-generation model, and switching on within one vendor lineage (Qwen3 -> Qwen3.6) at fixed MoE architecture and 3B active parameters; (ii) effective cutoffs span 22 months across vendors and precede vendor-reported dates by up to eight months, invalidating calendar-window placebo designs; (iii) audit results are not invariant to serving -- BF16 serving of an FP8-referenced model breaks the trigger estimate's stability while AWQ-INT4 preserves it, and a provider-locked reasoning regime makes one probe non-convergent -- so the protocol ships with operational requirements (pin quantization and thinking regime; disclose parser and sampling policy). We release the panel, frozen preregistrations, per-model audit rows with measured dollar costs, transcripts, and one-command regeneration.

</details>


### [187] [H$^2$SD: Hybrid Hindsight Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18955)
*Qiye Cai,Yichuan Ma,Linyang Li,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Qipeng Guo,Yicheng Zou,Tao Gui,Xiaocheng Feng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H²SD（混合事后自我蒸馏框架），根据轨迹正确性差异化使用教师信号：成功轨迹中教师仅调节更新幅度，失败轨迹中教师提供显式纠正方向。在多个推理基准上优于RLVR、OPSD和RLSD等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法使用稀疏的标量结果奖励，缺乏细粒度的token级信用分配。OPD需要额外教师模型且假设共享词汇表，OPSD通过特权信息构建教师但可能导致信息泄露和不稳定优化，RLSD无法在采样推理失败时提供显式纠正方向。为解决上述权衡，需要一种混合方法根据轨迹正确性差异化使用教师信号。

Method: H²SD框架根据轨迹正确性采用两种策略：1）成功轨迹：教师接收经确认正确的学生回答及改写指令，使用教师在原始响应token上的概率仅调节更新幅度，不改变奖励确定的更新方向；2）失败轨迹：教师接收包含关键推理步骤和验证答案的参考提示，最小化学生到教师的反向KL散度，提供显式纠正方向。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的推理基准上，H²SD持续优于代表性的RLVR、OPSD和RLSD基线，同时保持稳定的优化过程和良好的生成效率。

Conclusion: 通过根据轨迹正确性差异化使用教师信号，H²SD成功平衡了成功轨迹的稳定优化与失败轨迹的显式纠正，为强化学习中的token级监督提供了一种有效的混合方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning capabilities of large language models on tasks such as mathematical reasoning and code generation. However, most RLVR methods assign a scalar outcome reward to an entire trajectory, resulting in sparse supervision and limited token-level credit assignment. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides denser supervision by distilling token-level distributions from a stronger teacher model, but requires an additional teacher and typically assumes a shared vocabulary. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes this dependency by conditioning the same model on privileged information to construct a teacher policy. However, directly matching the teacher distribution may cause information leakage and unstable optimization. RLSD avoids direct matching by using the teacher signal only to modulate update magnitudes, but it cannot provide an explicit correction direction when the sampled reasoning fails. To address this tradeoff, we introduce $\mathrm{H}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$, a hybrid hindsight self distillation framework that uses the teacher differently according to trajectory correctness. For successful trajectories, the teacher receives the student response confirmed as correct together with a rephrasing instruction, and its probabilities on the original response tokens are used to modulate update magnitudes without changing the direction determined by the reward. For failed trajectories, we condition the teacher on a reference hint containing key reasoning steps and a verified answer, and minimize the reverse KL divergence from the student to the teacher. Experiments on multiple challenging reasoning benchmarks show that H$^2$SD consistently outperforms representative RLVR, OPSD, and RLSD baselines while maintaining stable optimization and favorable generation efficiency.

</details>


### [188] [Contraction-Gauge Preconditioning for Quantized Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18745)
*Piyush Sao,Narasinga Miniskar,Pedro Valero-Lara,Keita Teranishi,Sudip Seal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个低精度量化矩阵乘法的误差分析，提出了基于收缩规范预条件（contraction-gauge preconditioning）的误差缩减方法，并通过几何规划（GP）和线性规划对正对角规范族进行优化选择，实验证明该方法在图像分类器中显著降低了乘积误差和最终分类损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度矩阵乘法在深度学习推理中广泛应用，但量化引入的乘积误差尚缺乏精确的理论刻画和系统性的优化方法。现有方法多基于启发式策略，缺乏理论保证，亟需可解释、可优化的误差控制框架。

Method: 1) 推导了在独立零均值逐元素误差假设下的乘积误差精确有限维恒等式，适用于非过载减性抖动和独立随机舍入；2) 利用乘积保持等价变换 AB=(AT)(T^{-1}B)，提出收缩规范预条件——联合选择因子表示及其共享模式；3) 对正对角规范族（folds），用几何规划计算全局最优共享fold，用线性规划判断identity fold是否最优；4) 对其他规范族（缩放、划分、旋转、层次深度）推导可计算的选择统计量（尾指数、轮廓扩展度、相干性、加权Gram能量、切片能量协方差等），并给出上界用于排序候选。

Result: 在训练好的三块图像分类器的12个线性乘积上，抖动模型预测与确定性RTN误差的中位秩相关系数在8-bit为0.937、4-bit为0.918。GP fold相比identity fold在几何均值上降低了18.0%（8-bit）和20.5%（4-bit）的留出乘积误差，优于SmoothQuant风格网格基线，并在10/12个乘积上表现更好；组合logit MSE降低15.4%（8-bit）和26.4%（4-bit）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了精确的随机乘积误差核算、对角族内的认证选择方法，以及一个用于在RTN下评估可重用变换候选的通用目标函数。该方法兼具理论严谨性与实际有效性。

Abstract: We study low-precision computation of C=AB with both factors quantized. We derive an exact finite-dimensional identity for the expected squared product error under independent, zero-mean entrywise errors with known variance fields; it holds exactly for non-overloading subtractive dither and for independent stochastic rounding, and we empirically assess deterministic round-to-nearest (RTN). Using the product-preserving equivalence AB=(AT)(T^{-1}B), we formulate contraction-gauge preconditioning: jointly choosing a factor representation and its sharing pattern before quantization. Preconditioning can reduce product error but may require extra transformed, quantized copies of the opposite operand: a shared transform needs one copy, a block-specific transform up to one per block. Within the bounded family of positive diagonal gauges (folds), a geometric program computes a globally optimal shared fold and a linear program decides whether the identity fold is already optimal. For other families we derive computable selection statistics -- tail index for scaling, profile spread for partitioning, coherence and weighted-Gram energy for rotations, slice-energy covariance for hierarchy depth -- with upper bounds for ranking heuristic candidates. Across twelve linear products from a trained three-block image classifier, median within-product rank correlations between dither-model predictions and deterministic-RTN errors are 0.937 at 8 bits and 0.918 at 4 bits. The GP fold cuts held-out product error over the identity fold by 18.0% (8-bit) and 20.5% (4-bit) in geometric mean, beats a SmoothQuant-style grid baseline at both precisions and on ten of twelve products, and lowers composed logit MSE by 15.4% and 26.4%. We thus provide exact stochastic product-error accounting, certified selection within the diagonal family, and a common objective for evaluating reusable transform candidates under RTN.

</details>


### [189] [ConceptCF: Concept-based Counterfactuals for the Explainability of Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18748)
*Annemarie Jutte,Faizan Ahmed,Jeroen Linssen,Maurice van Keulen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptCF提出了一种基于可解释概念的时间序列反事实生成方法，通过时间序列分解构建概念（如尺度、频带），并用遗传算法优化概念变异，在多个指标上达到顶尖性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗、预测性维护）中，AI模型需要可解释性以确保依赖因果关系而非虚假相关。现有时间序列反事实方法在单个点或子序列上操作，无法确保变异的可解释性。

Method: 通过时间序列分解构建可解释概念（如尺度、频带），然后使用遗传算法优化概念层面的变异来生成反事实样本。

Result: 与五种最先进方法对比，ConceptCF在有效性、置信度、接近性、稀疏性和合理性等指标上均达到顶尖水平。

Conclusion: 基于概念的反事实生成方法能够提供更可解释的突变，同时在多项评估指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes ConceptCF, a method for counterfactual generation that operates on human-interpretable concepts. In high-stakes domains such as healthcare and predictive maintenance, artificial intelligence models can increase efficiency and safety. Explainability is key to ensure these models rely on causal relationships rather than spurious correlations. Counterfactual explanations identify minimal modifications that would change a model's predictions. Existing methods for time series operate on individual points or subsequences without ensuring interpretability of the mutations. ConceptCF instead modifies meaningful concepts. As a result we can provide explanations in terms of these concepts, for example ``the model's prediction would be `Sit' instead of `Walk' if you increase the scale of the movement''. In this paper, the concepts are constructed through time series decomposition, resulting in concepts such as scale, and frequency bands. Counterfactuals are generated using a genetic algorithm that optimizes the concept mutations. Evaluation against five state-of-the-art approaches demonstrates that ConceptCF consistently achieves top-tier performance across validity, confidence, proximity, sparsity and plausibility metrics.

</details>


### [190] [AdaFlash: Adaptive Speculative Decoding via On-Policy Distilled Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19223)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Hao-Cong Wu,Chen Chen,Jiacheng Sun,Zhenhua Dong,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散草稿模型的双向注意力机制虽然带来并行生成和全局建模能力，但也导致领域级和令牌级的高方差问题。为此提出AdaFlash框架，包括基于反向KL散度的在线策略蒸馏算法降低领域级方差，以及自适应长度头动态调整候选序列长度降低令牌级方差，在并发场景下吞吐量提升高达66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散草稿模型（如DFlash）的双向注意力机制是一把双刃剑：虽然赋予模型并行生成和全局上下文建模能力，但其固有的全局依赖性导致领域级和令牌级的高方差问题——不同领域的接受率波动大，不同令牌位置的草稿质量也参差不齐，制约了推测解码的效率。

Method: 提出AdaFlash框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 针对扩散草稿模型设计的基于反向KL散度的在线策略蒸馏算法，实现稳定收敛并有效降低领域级方差；(ii) 自适应长度头，能够动态调整候选序列长度，显著降低目标模型的验证成本并处理令牌级方差。

Result: 实验表明AdaFlash在部署时持续提升加速比，在高并发场景下效果尤为显著，吞吐量相比此前最先进方法提升高达约66%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性地解决扩散草稿模型双向注意力带来的方差问题，AdaFlash框架有效提升了推测解码的推理加速效率，尤其在高并发场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Speculative decoding, in which a lightweight draft model first generates a draft sequence that is then verified in parallel by the target model, has become a prevalent paradigm for accelerating large language model inference. Recent work such as DFlash further boosts drafting efficiency by leveraging diffusion drafters, whose parallel denoising mechanism enables draft generation in a single forward pass. In this work, we uncover a central pitfall of diffusion drafters: bidirectional attention is a double-edged sword. On one hand, it endows the model with parallel generation and global contextual modeling capabilities; on the other hand, this inherent global dependency introduces high variance at both the domain-level and the token-level: acceptance rates fluctuate substantially across different domains, and draft token quality also varies heterogeneously at different token positions. To tackle this issue, we propose AdaFlash framework, comprising two components: (i) an on-policy distillation (OPD) algorithm with reverse-KL divergence tailored for diffusion drafters, bringing stable convergence and effectively reducing domain-level variance; and (ii) an adaptive length head that dynamically adjusts the candidate sequence length on the fly, substantially lowering the verification cost of the target model and handling token-level variance. Experiments demonstrate that AdaFlash consistently improves speedup rate during deployment, with especially significant gains in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving up to approximately 66% higher throughput than previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [191] [Decafs: Disentangled Conditional adversarial Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18755)
*Anirudh jain,Sakshi Varshney,Samuel Kaski,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于李群的條件生成模型，通过对齐可解释的替代潜空间与流模型的潜空间，实现了可控生成，在图像和分子生成任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成模型潜空间纠缠不清，难以解释，限制了可控生成能力。

Method: 利用李群构建条件生成器，解耦替代潜空间，并通过对抗损失与流模型的潜空间对齐。

Result: 在MNIST和dSprites上超越StyleGAN，在QM9、ZINC和MOSES分子生成任务上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不增加流空间维度的前提下，实现了可解释的条件生成。

Abstract: Flow-based models have established state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling across domains, but are hard to interpret due to their complex latent embeddings. In particular, the entanglement of generative factors in the latent space hinders controlled generation. We circumvent this issue by appealing to a novel conditional generator based on Lie groups that disentangles an alternative latent space, which is aligned closely with the latent flow space using an adversarial loss. Our approach facilitates interpretable conditional generation while obviating the need to expand the dimensionality of the flow space (owing to its invertibility requirements). The proposed model demonstrates strong performance across conditional image (including, outperforming StyleGAN on MNIST, dSprites) and molecule (using standard QM9, ZINC and MOSES) generation tasks

</details>


### [192] [CircuitKIT : Circuit Discovery, Evaluation, and Application Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19317)
*Pratinav Seth,Hem Gosalia,Aditya Kasliwal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitKIT是一个开源的电路分析库，通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接电路分析工作流，提供发现算法、声明式接口、诊断工具和应用模块，统一了电路分析的发现、评估和干预流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电路分析方法存在碎片化问题：发现、评估和干预需要拼接独立的实现，且许多发现方法需要手动编写对比提示词。这使得方法难以比较，应用范围受限。

Method: CircuitKIT通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接电路分析工作流，提供一套发现算法、声明式接口（将结构化数据映射到发现任务）、补充电路诊断工具以及下游应用模块。

Result: 提供了一个完整的电路分析基础设施，包含统一的工具链，支持从发现到干预的完整工作流，并已开源发布。

Conclusion: CircuitKIT通过统一的基础设施简化了电路分析流程，使不同方法易于比较，并拓展了电路分析的应用范围。

Abstract: Circuit analysis can support not only model explanation but also downstream interventions such as pruning, editing, steering, and selective fine-tuning. However, conducting such analyses currently requires stitching together separate implementations for discovery, evaluation, and intervention, as well as hand-authoring the contrastive prompts required by many discovery methods. This fragmentation makes methods difficult to compare and limits their application beyond canonical tasks. We introduce CircuitKIT, a source-available library that connects the circuit-analysis workflow through a typed, serializable representation. CircuitKIT provides a suite of discovery algorithms, declarative interfaces for mapping structured data into discovery tasks, complementary circuit diagnostics, and downstream application modules. Together, these components provide common infrastructure for conducting and comparing circuit analyses. The library, examples, notebooks, and documentation are released at https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/CircuitKIT .

</details>


### [193] [Relative Positions Generalize, Absolute Positions Memorize: An Implicit-Bias Account of Length Generalization in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18759)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Transformer在序列长度泛化上的差异源于优化隐式偏差：旋转位置编码（RoPE）使注意力logit成为仅关于相对偏移的函数（精确等变性），因此在训练长度外完美复制选择规则；而绝对位置编码的训练头会锁定绝对位置，导致超出范围时无约束。研究还揭示了RoPE的“载波”核机制和注意力稀释定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的关于Transformer长度泛化能力的解释主要关注表达性（即是否存在长度泛化解），而本文旨在从优化角度解释这一现象——梯度下降在众多适合短序列的解中具体选择了哪一个，从而决定了外推能力。

Method: 在最小固定偏移检索任务上隔离位置选择机制，理论分析RoPE和绝对位置编码下注意力头的优化隐式偏差；证明RoPE的精确等变性；推导出RoPE的低秩“载波”核与注意力稀释定律，并在多层多头Transformer上验证。

Result: RoPE的注意力logit仅依赖相对偏移，使训练长度内学习的选择规则完美复制到更长序列；绝对位置编码则锁定绝对位置，超出范围无约束。RoPE的准确率衰减遵循注意力稀释定律。线性注意力控制实验表明，无softmax归一化时训练会选择最小范数插值解，无法外推。

Conclusion: 该优化解释将注意力隐式偏差、循环模型中外推的隐式偏差以及RASP-L猜想的学习层面联系起来，为理解Transformer长度泛化提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Transformers with relative positional encodings often extrapolate to sequences longer than those seen during training, whereas transformers with learned absolute encodings typically do not. This is a robust empirical regularity, and the explanations offered for it so far are chiefly about expressivity, that is, about whether a length-generalizing solution exists. We give an optimization explanation. On a minimal fixed-offset retrieval task that isolates positional selection, the gap is governed by the implicit bias of the trained attention head: among the many solutions that fit short sequences, which one gradient descent actually selects. We prove that rotary encodings make the attention logit a function of relative offset alone, an exact equivariance, so whatever selection rule is learned at training lengths is reproduced verbatim at every longer length. Learned absolute encodings instead leave out-of-range positions unconstrained, and the trained head pins to a fixed absolute position inside the training range. We characterize the learned rotary rule as a low-rank ``carrier'' kernel aligned with the target offset, and we derive the resulting graceful accuracy decay as an attention-dilution law; both predictions are confirmed across seeds and offsets. A linear-attention control shows the mechanism is specific to softmax: without normalization, training selects a min-norm interpolant that does not extrapolate. The phenomenon, the equivariance, and the carrier all transfer to a multi-layer, multi-head transformer trained on a full-sequence length-generalization task. The account connects the implicit bias of attention, implicit bias for extrapolation in recurrent models, and the learning side of the RASP-L conjecture.

</details>


### [194] [PertReason: A Knowledge-Grounded Benchmark and Framework for Cell-State-Conditioned Mechanistic Reasoning of Perturbation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18777)
*Dongkwan Kim,Yiming Gao,Yining Yang,Yang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PertReason框架，包含基准PertReasonQA和模型PertReasonLM，用于评估和提升机器学习在单细胞扰动效应推理中的机制忠实性，暴露了当前模型在分布漂移下预测准确性与机械推理之间的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习模型在科学领域的评估中，往往只关注预测准确性，而忽略了在真实分布漂移（如新细胞类型、未见扰动）下推理的机制忠实性，导致模型可能通过错误逻辑得到正确答案而无法被发现。

Method: 构建PertReasonQA基准：整合单细胞遗传和化学扰动数据、知识图谱，动态条件化细胞特异性基态通路以避免记忆。提出PertReasonLM：训练大语言模型将结果预测与上下文特异性机械推理对齐，通过将理由植根于上下文特异性通路并收紧结果与机制之间的一致性来针对已识别的失败模式。

Result: 评估发现最先进的模型存在系统性差距，表现为通过错误逻辑得出正确答案、忽略细胞上下文、生成方向不一致的机制等，这些失败模式在标准基准中基本不可见。

Conclusion: PertReason框架为数据丰富的科学系统中暴露和缓解忠实推理失败提供了诊断工具，强调了在科学领域评估中分离正确预测与正确推理的必要性。

Abstract: Evaluating machine learning in scientific domains requires separating correct predictions from correct reasons under realistic distribution shifts. We introduce PertReason, a knowledge-grounded benchmark and framework suite for cell-state--conditioned reasoning about perturbation effects. At its core, PertReasonQA is a benchmark that tests whether models can generate mechanistically faithful explanations while remaining robust to complex shifts, such as new cells and unseen perturbations. PertReasonQA combines single-cell genetic and chemical perturbation data across multiple cellular contexts with knowledge graphs, and dynamically conditions pathways on cell-specific basal states to avoid generic memorization. Evaluations on state-of-the-art models reveal systematic gaps between predictive accuracy and mechanistic reasoning. Specifically, these models exhibit failure modes largely invisible to standard benchmarks, such as deriving correct answers through flawed logic, ignoring cellular context, and generating directionally inconsistent mechanisms. As a reference probe of the benchmark, we present PertReasonLM, a large language model trained to align outcome predictions with context-specific mechanistic reasoning. Our model targets the identified failure modes by grounding rationales in context-specific pathways and tightening agreement between outcomes and mechanisms. Together, we provide a diagnostic framework for exposing and mitigating failures in faithful reasoning in data-rich scientific systems.

</details>


### [195] [QScheduler: Adaptive Gradient Sampling for Zeroth-Order On-Device Training on INT8 NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18802)
*Victor Felipe Domingues Do Amaral,Pierre Demaj,Erwan Libessart,Laurent Folliot,Anthony Kolar,Philippe Bénabès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QScheduler是一种自适应算法，能根据训练进度动态调整零阶优化中的梯度采样数量q，无需超参数搜索，并在STM32N6的Neural-ART NPU上首次实现了INT8量化设备端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化中梯度采样数量q的选择至关重要：样本太少导致梯度噪声大、训练早停，样本太多则浪费计算资源。然而，寻找最优q通常需要昂贵的超参数搜索，缺乏自适应调整机制。

Method: 提出QScheduler自适应算法，根据训练进度动态调整零阶优化中的梯度采样数量q，并在STM32N6的Neural-ART NPU上首次实现INT8量化设备端训练。

Result: 在EuroSAT和STL-10数据集上，使用ResNet18和MobileNetV2，QScheduler在不需预先优化超参数q的情况下，性能匹配精心调优的固定q配置。

Conclusion: QScheduler有效消除了零阶优化中q的手动调优需求，为资源受限的NPU设备端学习提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization enables On-Device Learning (ODL) on NPU-equipped microcontrollers by estimating gradients through forward passes alone, bypassing the need for backpropagation primitives and reducing memory requirements. The number of gradient samples q critically affects training: insufficient samples produce noisy gradients that plateau early, while excessive samples consume more computational resources. However, finding an optimal q typically requires costly hyperparameter searches. This work introduces QScheduler, an adaptive algorithm that adjusts q based on training progress, and provides the first proof-of-concept of INT8 quantized on-device training on the STM32N6's Neural-ART NPU. Experiments on EuroSAT and STL-10 show that QScheduler matches well-tuned fixed-q configurations for both ResNet18 and MobileNetV2, without requiring prior q hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [196] [Elicitation without Backpropagation: Steering Model Behavior by Optimizing the Latent Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18804)
*Garrett Baker,Vinayak Pathak,Daniel Murfet,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出后验前缀调优（PPT），一种无需反向传播即可从变压器中引出高期望效用行为的方法，通过在贝叶斯滤波变压器（BFT）上优化硬提示分布，利用潜在后验模型和重要性采样实现高效的多效用优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用变压器行为的潜在后验模型，在贝叶斯滤波变压器（BFT）这一精确场景下，探索如何通过提示工程高效地引导模型生成高期望效用的续写内容，避免传统方法需要大量前向传播和反向传播的计算开销。

Method: 提出后验前缀调优（PPT），通过优化硬提示分布来引导BFT行为。方法包括：(1) 利用预测蒙特卡洛（PMC）从BFT中一次性采样先验样本；(2) 通过重要性采样估计效用的梯度；(3) 优化过程无需任何变压器前向传播和反向传播，且先验样本与效用无关，可重复用于多种效用函数。

Result: 在Beta-Bernoulli和强化瓮BFT上，针对反向交叉熵、频率匹配和Dyck有效性三类效用函数验证了PPT的有效性。

Conclusion: PPT提供了一种高效、可扩展的提示优化方法，能够在无需额外计算开销的情况下，针对多种效用函数从变压器中引出期望行为。

Abstract: In the \emph{latent posterior model} of transformer behavior, the next-token distribution arises from a posterior over latent predictive models conditioned on the context, mixed to generate continuations. We exploit this model in settings where it is exact, namely Bayes-filtered transformers (BFTs) meta-learned on sequences from a hierarchical prior, to introduce \textbf{Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)}, a new method for \emph{eliciting} behavior from a transformer: given a utility function on continuations, find a prompt under which the transformer generates continuations of high expected utility. For a BFT, the elicitation objective factors through the latent posterior, and the gradient of this objective can be estimated from samples of the prior alone. PPT optimizes the parameters of a distribution over hard prompts: it draws prior samples once from the BFT via predictive Monte Carlo (PMC), then estimates the gradient by importance sampling against them. The optimization performs no transformer forward passes and no backpropagation through the transformer, and the prior samples are utility-independent, so a single set of samples drives elicitation against any number of utilities at negligible marginal cost. We validate PPT on Beta--Bernoulli and reinforced urn BFTs across three utility families (reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity).

</details>


### [197] [Countercurrent Multiplier Networks: A Renal-Inspired Iterative Operator with Provably Bounded Fixed-Point Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18829)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将哺乳动物肾脏中用于浓缩尿液的逆流倍增机制形式化为一种可微分的序列算子——逆流倍增（CCM）层，并研究其作为残差迭代精制替代方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经架构中缺乏与肾脏逆流倍增机制类似的有效梯度放大机制，作者希望借鉴这一生物机制来改进深度学习中的迭代精制过程。

Method: 将逆流倍增机制形式化为可微分的CCM层，该层通过两个反平行流在发夹点连接，利用弱有界局部泵循环放大为大的轴向梯度，作为序列算子应用于神经网络中。

Result: CCM层能够像肾脏一样，通过局部小幅效应实现全局大幅梯度放大，从不超过200 mOsm的单效应梯度实现四倍浓度增加。

Conclusion: 逆流倍增机制可以作为残差迭代精制的有效替代方案，为神经架构设计提供新的生物启发式思路。

Abstract: The mammalian kidney concentrates urine using a mechanism with no analogue in current neural architectures: the countercurrent multiplier. Two anti-parallel flows joined at a hairpin recirculate a weak magnitude-bounded local pump into a large axial gradient achieving a four-fold concentration increase from a single-effect gradient that never exceeds 200 mOsm at any point. We formalize this mechanism as a differentiable sequence operator the Countercurrent Multiplier (CCM) layer and study it as an alternative to residual iterative refinement.

</details>


### [198] [From Trajectories to Instructions: Language-Conditioned Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18830)
*Garvit Singla,Uma Maheswari Natarajan,Raghuram Bharadwaj Diddigi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LA-MAML，利用语言指令替代MAML内循环中的轨迹收集和梯度更新，通过单步嵌入任务指令来适应全局策略参数，在BabyAI基准上取得了与基线相当或更优的性能，同时大幅降低了每次迭代的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAML的内循环需要收集任务环境轨迹并执行梯度更新，计算成本高昂。作者注意到外循环才是驱动全局参数学习的关键，因此内循环适应机制不必局限于梯度方法。

Method: 提出LA-MAML，利用任务附带的自然语言指令作为任务特定信号，通过学习指令嵌入，在内循环中以单步方式直接调整全局策略参数，替代原有的轨迹收集和基于梯度的更新过程。

Result: 在BabyAI基准上，LA-MAML在性能上与基线方法相当或更优，同时显著降低了每次迭代的壁钟训练时间。

Conclusion: 语言指令可以作为元强化学习中基于轨迹的内循环适应的有效且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML) is a widely used framework for reinforcement learning (RL) that enables efficient transfer by learning global policy parameters that can be rapidly adapted to new tasks. MAML training proceeds in two loops: an inner loop where the global parameters are adapted to task-specific parameters, and an outer loop where these task-specific parameters are evaluated and losses are back-propagated to improve the global parameters. Traditionally, the inner loop adaptation is performed by collecting trajectories from the task environment and applying gradient updates on the empirical expected return, which can be a costly operation. We note that it is the outer loop that drives the actual learning of global parameters, and therefore the inner loop adaptation mechanism need not be restricted to be gradient-based. This observation leads us to ask: Can we replace the inner loop trajectory collection and gradient update with a simpler, task-specific signal? In many practical settings, tasks are naturally accompanied by language instructions. Leveraging these instructions as a direct task-specific signal, we propose LA-MAML (Language Adapted MAML), which modifies the inner loop by adapting the global policy parameters in a single step through a learned embedding of the task instruction, replacing the inner loop trajectory collection and gradient-based updates. Experiments on the BabyAI benchmark demonstrate that LA-MAML achieves competitive or improved performance compared to baselines at a significantly lower per-iteration wall-clock training time. These results demonstrate that language instructions are an effective and efficient substitute for trajectory-based inner loop adaptation in meta RL.

</details>


### [199] [ABOPD: Antibody CDR Design via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18835)
*Zhuo Yang,Jiaying He,Jiaqing Xie,Daolang Wang,Xipeng Qiu,Yuxin Wang,Tianfan Fu,Beilun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ABOPD是一个基于策略蒸馏的抗体设计框架，通过在训练中利用原生几何结构监督模型自身的去噪轨迹，显著提升了抗体CDR-H3环的结构恢复精度（RMSD从2.37Å降至1.95Å）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质生成模型的标准去噪训练使用扰动原生结构获得的噪声状态，而递归生成经过模型生成的中间状态，这种不匹配导致柔性抗体CDR环（如CDR-H3）的骨架偏差沿去噪轨迹累积，损害抗原面对接环的几何结构。

Method: 提出ABOPD框架，采用基于策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）的方法，在训练过程中利用特权原生几何结构（privileged native geometry）监督模型自身去噪轨迹上所访问的状态。

Result: 在RAbD基准上的CDR-H3生成任务中，RMSD从2.37Å降至1.95Å（降低0.42Å），优于监督微调和离线蒸馏对照方法。

Conclusion: ABOPD通过细粒度结构监督有效解决了训练与推理之间的去噪轨迹不匹配问题，为高保真蛋白质设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Antibodies are essential therapeutic molecules, and their complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) form the primary antigen-recognition interface. Recent protein generative models have demonstrated broad capabilities in biomolecular design, yet post-training strategies for downstream objectives remain limited. Standard denoising training operates on noisy states obtained by perturbing native structures, whereas recursive generation proceeds through model-generated intermediate states. For flexible antibody CDR loops such as CDR-H3, this mismatch can allow backbone deviations to accumulate along the denoising trajectory and compromise antigen-facing loop geometry. We introduce ABOPD, an antibody design framework based on on-policy distillation that leverages privileged native geometry during training to supervise states visited along the model's own denoising trajectories. With this fine-grained structural supervision, ABOPD substantially improves structural recovery on RAbD CDR-H3 generation, reducing RMSD by 0.42 Å (from 2.37 Å to 1.95 Å) and outperforming supervised fine-tuning and offline distillation controls, offering a path to higher-fidelity protein design.

</details>


### [200] [Regime-Aware Physics-Guided Early Warning of Lithium-Ion Battery Thermal Runaway Using Thermo-Mechanical Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18860)
*Syed Sajid Ullah,Muhammad Zunair Zamir,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向锂离子电池热失控的、融合热-力传感信息的物理引导框架，通过轻量级卷积分类器推断安全/警告/危险状态，并利用因果时序卷积网络、特征调制和物理偏置注意力实现早期预警。在30组机械滥用测试中，该方法F1达0.89，预警提前量达15.6秒，比最优基线提升69.6%，去除力信号后提前量下降60.3%，验证了机械前兆信号的关键价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热失控预警方法主要依赖温度信号，忽略了热失控发生前就已出现的机械前兆（如力、形变），导致预警滞后。因此，需要融合热-力多模态信号以实现更早、更可靠的预警。

Method: 提出一个状态感知的物理引导框架：1) 轻量级卷积分类器从机械信号推断安全/警告/危险状态；2) 状态估计通过特征线性调制、物理偏置注意力和状态相关门控来调节因果时序卷积骨干网络；3) 联合学习统一了状态识别、热失控检测和事故时间估计三项任务。

Result: 在30组机械滥用测试（10%、50%、90% SOC，两种加载协议）上，F1=0.89，高温预测RMSE=12.3°C，平均预警提前量15.6秒，检测成功率0.92，实验级误报率2.7%。预警提前量比最强基线高69.6%，去除力信号后提前量下降60.3%。

Conclusion: 状态感知的热-力融合策略在受控机械滥用条件下能实现更早、更可靠的热失控预警，机械前兆信号对提升预警提前量至关重要。

Abstract: Thermal runaway in lithium-ion batteries poses a major safety risk to electric vehicles and energy storage systems. Current early-warning methods depend mainly on temperature and may therefore miss mechanical precursors that emerge before rapid heating. We introduce a regime-aware, physics-guided framework that integrates temperature, voltage, force, deformation, and state-of-charge measurements for early warning under controlled mechanical abuse. A lightweight convolutional classifier first infers safe, warning, or danger regimes from mechanical signals. These regime estimates then condition a causal temporal convolutional backbone through feature-wise linear modulation, physics-biased attention, and regime-dependent gating. Joint learning unifies regime identification, thermal-runaway detection, and time-to-disaster estimation. We evaluate the framework using leave-one-experiment-out cross-validation on 30 mechanical-abuse tests across state-of-charge levels of 10%, 50%, and 90% and two loading protocols. The method achieves an F1 score of 0.89, a high-temperature prediction root-mean-square error of 12.3 °C, a mean warning lead time of 15.6 s, a detection success rate of 0.92, and an experiment-level false alarm rate of 2.7%. Its lead time exceeds that of the strongest baseline by 69.6%. Removing force reduces the lead time by 60.3%, highlighting the value of mechanical precursors. These results support regime-aware thermo-mechanical fusion as a promising strategy for earlier and more reliable thermal-runaway warning under controlled abuse conditions.

</details>


### [201] [Reinforcement Learning for Delivery Drone-Based Participatory Sensing in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18874)
*Xin Ouyang,Songxin Lei,Xusen Guo,Yutian Jiang,Sijie Ruan,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSRL框架，通过双层强化学习（宏观任务调度+微观速度控制）解决无人机城市感知中的环境干扰（如风力）和可扩展性问题，在实际数据集上实现20.1%-46.6%的系统收益提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机感知方法忽视风力等环境扰动对速度和能效的影响，且面临无人机规模扩大时的可扩展性瓶颈以及宏观任务调度与微观速度控制之间的多时间尺度决策异构问题。

Method: 提出双时间尺度强化学习（TSRL）框架：(1)宏观层：任务嵌入感知调度器，通过独立编码任务特征并顺序评估无人机适配性来处理可扩展性；(2)微观层：风感知速度控制器，学习细粒度速度调度以适应环境变化。

Result: 在杭州和上海的真实数据集上，TSRL分别实现20.1%和46.6%的平均系统收益提升，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TSRL通过分离宏观调度与微观控制的决策时间尺度，有效解决了动态环境下无人机联合配送与感知的可扩展性和环境适应性难题。

Abstract: Using Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) for urban sensing has emerged as a powerful paradigm to monitor the status of the city, e.g., air quality and noise levels, through agile aerial crowdsourcing. Despite this potential, existing UAV-based sensing approaches overlook environmental disturbances like wind that drastically impact drone velocity and energy efficiency. Consequently, directly applying existing methods to this joint delivery and sensing paradigm in dynamic environments faces two severe challenges: (1) scalability bottlenecks as fleet sizes expand; and (2) multi-timescale decision heterogeneity between macro task dispatching and micro velocity control. To tackle these, we formalize the problem as SensUAV and propose a Two TimeScale Reinforcement Learning framework (TSRL). Specifically, TSRL separates decision-making into two cooperative layers. At the macro level, a task-embedding sensing dispatcher handles scalability by separately encoding distinct task features and sequentially evaluating UAV suitability before task selection. At the micro level, a wind-aware velocity controller learns fine-grained velocity scheduling to adapt to dynamic environmental variations. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TSRL significantly outperforms baselines, achieving average system profit improvements of 20.1% in Hangzhou and 46.6% in Shanghai.

</details>


### [202] [Physics-Informed Super-Resolution of Atmospheric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18877)
*Chang Xu,Gencer Sumbul,Hugo Porta,Manon Béchaz,Sebastian Schemm,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息约束的超分辨率方法（PISR），通过将流体静力学原始方程融入深度学习模型，提升大气数据降尺度的物理一致性和极端事件检测能力，并提出了归一化物理一致性（NPC）指标来量化超分辨结果的物理可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖背景下极端事件日益频发，需要高分辨率大气数据进行可靠检测和预测；但现有观测数据空间分辨率不足，而现有机器学习超分辨率方法虽然高效，却无法保证重建数据满足地球系统的基本物理定律，降低了其在气候应用中的可信度。

Method: 提出物理信息超分辨率（PISR）方法，将多尺度物理信息约束（基于流体静力学原始方程）融入超分辨率模型；同时提出归一化物理一致性（NPC）指标，用于定量评估超分辨率结果的物理一致性。

Result: 在ERA5、CERRA和COSMO数据集上的实验表明，PISR相比基准方法在物理一致性、超分辨率精度以及极端事件（热浪、极端风）下游检测任务上均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 将原始方程作为物理约束引入超分辨率模型，能够有效提升大气数据降尺度的物理可信度，增强对极端气候事件的检测能力，为气候应用中的可信赖AI方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: In the context of global warming, extreme events have become more frequent and intense, making their trustworthy detection and forecasting more important than ever. Yet, atmospheric observations lack sufficient spatial resolution, motivating atmospheric data downscaling as a way to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse observations. This task is now being formulated as a super-resolution (SR) problem with machine learning methods featuring high efficiency. Nevertheless, it remains unclear whether the super-resolved atmospheric data still satisfies fundamental physics governing the Earth system, raising concerns about their trustworthiness in climate-related applications. In this work, we address this challenge by constraining SR models to respect hydrostatic primitive equations that represent multivariate atmospheric physics. First, we propose a Physics-Informed Super-Resolution (PISR) method involving multi-scale physics-informed objectives based on primitive equations. PISR favors the SR outputs to respect these equations and therefore naturally encodes inter-variable relationships. In addition, we propose a metric called Normalized Physical Consistency (NPC) derived from said primitive equations to measure the physical consistency of super-resolved data. Experiments on ERA5, CERRA, and COSMO demonstrate that PISR enhances the reconstruction fidelity by improving physical consistency, SR accuracy, and downstream detection of extreme events, as demonstrated by case studies in heatwaves and extreme winds.

</details>


### [203] [RAMP: Recognition parametrisation by Amortised Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18883)
*Lior Fox,Kai Biegun,James Heald,Samo Hromadka,Arielle Rosinski,Maneesh Sahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAMP是一种基于识别参数化建模范式的新方法，通过学习灵活的非线性摊销消息传递框架隐式定义潜在结构，能够在复杂高维数据中高效恢复潜在变量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督学习的核心目标是揭示解释观测数据间依赖关系的潜在因素，但现有概率模型在学习潜在变量时面临两难：要么依赖可进行易处理信度传播的分布假设，要么使用随模型规模和复杂度扩展性差的近似方法。

Method: 基于识别参数化建模范式（recognition-parametrised modelling paradigm），提出RAMP方法，通过学习一个灵活的非线性摊销消息传递框架来隐式定义潜在结构。

Result: RAMP能够在表达性强的非线性模型中高效地基于似然恢复潜在变量分布，并适用于复杂高维数据。

Conclusion: RAMP为无监督学习中的潜在变量建模提供了一种替代性高效方案，避免了传统方法在易处理性和可扩展性之间的权衡。

Abstract: A central aim of unsupervised learning is to uncover latent factors that explain dependencies among observations. Probabilistic models typically achieve this by introducing multiple latent variables linked through a graph of conditional relationships, with distributional parameters and their dependence learnt from data. Learning relies either on distributional choices that allow tractable belief propagation, or on approximations that scale poorly with model size and complexity. We build on the recently developed recognition-parametrised modelling paradigm to propose an alternative approach: RAMP, a method that implicitly defines latent structure by learning a flexible, nonlinear, amortised message-passing framework. We show that RAMP enables efficient likelihood-based recovery of latent-variable distributions within expressive nonlinear models acting on complex high-dimensional data.

</details>


### [204] [KALE: Kernel Alignment with Loss Equilibration for Stable CLIP-DINOv2 Alignment at Web Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18885)
*Michał Pawłowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KUEA（基于核的CLIP与视觉中心教师DINOv2对齐）在ImageNet-1K上有效，但在网络规模数据CC12M上失败，因为对齐项梯度几乎消失。本文提出KALE，一种损失均衡控制器，自适应缩放对齐权重（需增加约四个数量级），无需逐数据集调参。在CC12M子集上，KALE保持图文检索，提升SVHN线性探测，零样本11数据集平均超过CLIP +2.00，优于KUEA的+1.29。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KUEA在ImageNet-1K上通过固定权衡权重有效对齐CLIP与DINOv2，但该方法在噪声更大、规模更大的网络数据（CC12M）上失效，因为对齐项梯度贡献降至约0.2%，信号几乎消失。现有固定权重策略无法适应不同数据分布，需要一种无需逐数据集调参的自适应方法。

Method: 提出KALE（损失均衡控制器），通过跟踪两类损失并自适应缩放对齐权重，使其达到目标比率，从而恢复梯度信号。具体地，控制器动态调整权重（需增加约四个数量级），配合有界高学习率和带适中下界的衰减调度，确保训练稳定且均衡收敛。

Result: 在330万图像的CC12M子集上，KALE保持图文检索性能，SVHN线性探测可重复提升；零样本11数据集平均提升+2.00（超过CLIP），优于KUEA的+1.29。所有结果报告了运行间方差，结论基于跨运行稳定的指标。

Conclusion: 固定权重对齐策略无法泛化到网络规模数据，KALE的自适应损失均衡机制有效恢复了梯度信号，无需逐数据集调参，且性能优于手工调优的固定权重方法。

Abstract: Kernel-based alignment of CLIP toward a vision centric teacher such as DINOv2 (KUEA) improves CLIP's visual representations while preserving text-encoder compatibility, using a fixed trade-off weight tuned on curated ImageNet-1K. We ask whether this transfers to noisy, web-scale data (CC12M) and find that it does not: the alignment term's weighted contribution falls to about 0.2% of the clean term, so under any fixed weight its gradient is effectively inert. We introduce KALE, a loss-equilibration controller that tracks both losses and adaptively rescales the alignment weight toward a target ratio, restoring the signal with no per-dataset tuning; reaching balance requires increasing the weight by roughly four orders of magnitude, and the required value is configuration-dependent, so no fixed scalar suffices. We characterize the resulting regime: a bounded high learning rate and a decaying schedule with a moderate floor are needed for stability, and the controller equilibrates rather than diverging. On a 3.3M-image CC12M subset, the aligned model preserves image-text retrieval and reproducibly improves SVHN linear probing; zero-shot improves by +2.00 over CLIP on the standard 11-dataset average, exceeding KUEA's +1.29. We report all results with explicit run-to-run variance and base our conclusions on the metrics that are stable across runs.

</details>
